798: MinimapSide active_minimap_side(const ClientState& state) {
799:   // Preserve the player's side preference, but temporarily yield the right
800:   // rail to the inventory diptych. Closing gear restores the chosen side.
801:   if (state.minimap_mode == MinimapMode::Corner && state.gear_overlay &&
802:       state.minimap_side == MinimapSide::Right)
803:     return MinimapSide::Left;
804:   return state.minimap_side;
805: }
806: 
807: std::string minimap_preferences_path() {
808:   char local_data[MAX_PATH]{};
809:   const DWORD length =
810:       GetEnvironmentVariableA("LOCALAPPDATA", local_data, MAX_PATH);
811:   if (length == 0 || length >= MAX_PATH) return {};
812:   const std::string directory = std::string(local_data) + "\\Verdigris";
813:   if (!CreateDirectoryA(directory.c_str(), nullptr) &&
814:       GetLastError() != ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS)
815:     return {};
816:   return directory + "\\client-map.ini";
817: }
818: 
819: bool write_minimap_preferences(const ClientState& state,
820:                                const std::string& path) {
821:   const std::string temporary = path + ".tmp";
822:   std::ofstream out(temporary, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
823:   if (!out) return false;
824:   out << "side="
825:       << (state.minimap_side == MinimapSide::Right ? "right" : "left")
826:       << "\nzoom=" << state.minimap_zoom_step
827:       << "\nopacity=" << state.minimap_opacity_step << "\n";
828:   out.flush();
829:   const bool complete = static_cast<bool>(out);
830:   out.close();
831:   if (!complete ||
832:       !MoveFileExA(temporary.c_str(), path.c_str(),
833:                    MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING | MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH)) {
834:     (void)DeleteFileA(temporary.c_str());
835:     return false;
836:   }
837:   return true;
838: }
839: 
840: bool read_minimap_preferences(ClientState& state, const std::string& path) {
841:   std::ifstream in(path, std::ios::binary);
842:   if (!in) return false;
843:   std::string line;
844:   const auto read_step = [](const std::string& value, int current) {
845:     char* end = nullptr;
846:     const long parsed = std::strtol(value.c_str(), &end, 10);
847:     if (end == value.c_str() || !end || *end != '\0') return current;
848:     if (parsed < 0) return 0;
849:     if (parsed >= kMinimapSettingSteps) return kMinimapSettingSteps - 1;
850:     return static_cast<int>(parsed);
851:   };
852:   while (std::getline(in, line)) {
853:     const std::size_t split = line.find('=');
854:     if (split == std::string::npos) continue;
855:     const std::string key = line.substr(0, split);
856:     const std::string value = line.substr(split + 1);
857:     if (key == "side") {
858:       if (value == "right") state.minimap_side = MinimapSide::Right;
859:       if (value == "left") state.minimap_side = MinimapSide::Left;
860:     } else if (key == "zoom") {
861:       state.minimap_zoom_step = read_step(value, state.minimap_zoom_step);
862:     } else if (key == "opacity") {
863:       state.minimap_opacity_step =
864:           read_step(value, state.minimap_opacity_step);
865:     }
866:   }
867:   return true;
868: }
869: 
870: void save_minimap_preferences(const ClientState& state) {
871:   if (!state.persist_minimap_preferences) return;
872:   const std::string path = minimap_preferences_path();
873:   if (!path.empty()) (void)write_minimap_preferences(state, path);
874: }
875: 
876: void load_minimap_preferences(ClientState& state) {
877:   const std::string path = minimap_preferences_path();
878:   if (!path.empty()) (void)read_minimap_preferences(state, path);
879:   // Only real interactive clients persist subsequent changes. Scenario
880:   // states never call this loader and therefore cannot touch user settings.
881:   state.persist_minimap_preferences = true;
--- THEIRS ---

14012: // Compact contract for the unfinished information-density pass: the XP strip
14013: // renders the supplied authoritative fraction, hover exposes a world actor,
14014: // and modal panes suppress world tooltips instead of drawing through them.
14015: int scenario_hud_information() {
14016:   ClientState state;
14017:   scenario_begin(state);
14018:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
14019:   sync_world(state);
14020:   state.world.xp_present = true;
14021:   state.world.xp_fraction = 0.42;
14022: 
14023:   constexpr int width = 960;
14024:   constexpr int height = 600;
14025:   HDC dc = CreateCompatibleDC(nullptr);
14026:   HBITMAP bitmap = CreateCompatibleBitmap(dc, width, height);
14027:   scenario_check(bitmap != nullptr,
14028:                  "hud-information: presentation surface allocated");
14029:   if (!bitmap) {
14030:     DeleteDC(dc);
14031:     return scenario_failures;
14032:   }
14033:   HGDIOBJ old = SelectObject(dc, bitmap);
14034:   RECT bounds{0, 0, width, height};
14035:   skin::set_ui_scale(hud_scale(height));
14036:   SelectObject(dc, skin::font_body());
14037:   render::List hud_ops;
14038:   paint_xp_bar(state, dc, bounds, hud_ops);
14039: 
14040:   const render::Item* xp = nullptr;
14041:   bool raster_xp = false;
14042:   for (const auto& item : hud_ops)
14043:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "xp-bar")
14044:       xp = &item;
14045:     else if (item.label == "framekit-raster:xp-rail")
14046:       raster_xp = true;
14047:   scenario_check(xp && xp->value == 42,
14048:                  "hud-information: XP strip records authoritative progress");
14049:   scenario_check(raster_xp,
14050:                  "hud-information: XP progress uses the authored WIZARD rail");
14051: 
14052:   scenario_check(!state.world.monsters.empty(),
14053:                  "hud-information: seeded route supplies a hover target");
14054:   if (!state.world.monsters.empty()) {
14055:     const WorldActor& monster = state.world.monsters.front();
14056:     const ScreenPoint base =
14057:         project(state.camera, bounds, monster.position.x, monster.position.y);
14058:     state.mouse.x = base.x;
14059:     state.mouse.y =
14060:         base.y - static_cast<int>(kTileUnits * 0.7 * base.scale);
14061:     paint_hover_tooltip(state, dc, bounds, hud_ops);
14062:   }
14063:   bool saw_tooltip = false;
14064:   bool raster_tooltip = false;
14065:   for (const auto& item : hud_ops)
14066:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("tooltip:", 0) == 0)
14067:       saw_tooltip = true;
14068:     else if (item.label == "framekit-raster:tooltip-frame")
14069:       raster_tooltip = true;
14070:   scenario_check(saw_tooltip,
14071:                  "hud-information: world hover emits a readable tooltip");
14072:   scenario_check(raster_tooltip,
14073:                  "hud-information: hover uses the authored WIZARD tooltip frame");
14074: 
14075:   state.gear_overlay = true;
14076:   render::List modal_rl;
14077:   paint_hover_tooltip(state, dc, bounds, modal_rl);
14078:   bool leaked_tooltip = false;
14079:   for (const auto& item : modal_rl)
14080:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("tooltip:", 0) == 0)
14081:       leaked_tooltip = true;
14082:   scenario_check(!leaked_tooltip,
14083:                  "hud-information: modal pane suppresses world hover");
14084: 
14085:   SelectObject(dc, old);
14086:   DeleteObject(bitmap);
14087:   DeleteDC(dc);
14088:   return 0;
14089: }

14353:   if (capture_override < 0) {
14354:     scenario_check(false,
14355:                    "character-inventory: capture root rejected before any write");
14356:     return 0;
14357:   }
14358:   if (capture_override == 0) {
14359:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
14360:     capture_dir = "captures";
14361:   }
14362:   for (const auto size : {POINT{960, 600}, POINT{1366, 768}}) {
14363:     const std::string capture_path =
14364:         capture_dir + "\\character-inventory-diptych-" +
14365:         std::to_string(size.x) + "x" + std::to_string(size.y) + ".png";
14366:     scenario_check(reference_present(state, size.x, size.y, capture_path),
14367:                    "character-inventory: Framekit diptych captured");
14368:     std::printf("    capture: %s\n", capture_path.c_str());
14369:     const HudRect expected_character = character_pane_rect(size.x, size.y);
14370:     const HudRect expected_gear = gear_pane_rect(size.x, size.y);
14371:     scenario_check(!hud_rects_overlap(expected_character, expected_gear),
14372:                    "character-inventory: paper doll and backpack do not overlap");
14373:     int seats = 0;
14374:     int filled = 0;
14375:     int footprints = 0;
14376:     int skill_rasters = 0;
14377:     bool ornate_panel = false;
14378:     bool life_orb = false;
14379:     bool resource_orb = false;
14380:     bool xp_rail = false;
14381:     bool bounded = true;
14382:     const HudRect* grid = nullptr;
14383:     for (const auto& trace : state.hud_rect_trace) {
14384:       if (trace.first == "paperdoll-seat") {
14385:         ++seats;
14386:         bounded = bounded && trace.second.x >= expected_character.x &&
14387:                   trace.second.y >= expected_character.y &&
14388:                   trace.second.x + trace.second.w <=
14389:                       expected_character.x + expected_character.w &&
14390:                   trace.second.y + trace.second.h <=
14391:                       expected_character.y + expected_character.h;
14392:       }
14393:       if (trace.first == "pane-grid") grid = &trace.second;
14394:       if (trace.first == "pane-item-footprint") ++footprints;
14395:     }
14396:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
14397:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
14398:           item.label.rfind("paperdoll:", 0) == 0 && item.value == 1) {
14399:         ++filled;
14400:       } else if (item.label.rfind("framekit-skill:", 0) == 0) {
14401:         ++skill_rasters;
14402:       } else if (item.label == "framekit-raster:panel-tiled-fill") {
14403:         ornate_panel = true;
14404:       } else if (item.label == "framekit-raster:orb-life") {
14405:         life_orb = true;
14406:       } else if (item.label == "framekit-raster:orb-resource") {
14407:         resource_orb = true;
14408:       } else if (item.label == "framekit-raster:xp-rail") {
14409:         xp_rail = true;
14410:       }
14411:     if (grid) {
14412:       for (const auto& trace : state.hud_rect_trace)
14413:         if (trace.first == "pane-item-footprint")
14414:           bounded = bounded && trace.second.x >= grid->x &&
14415:                     trace.second.y >= grid->y &&
14416:                     trace.second.x + trace.second.w <= grid->x + grid->w &&
14417:                     trace.second.y + trace.second.h <= grid->y + grid->h;
14418:     } else {
14419:       bounded = false;
14420:     }
14421:     scenario_check(seats == 11 && filled == 9,
14422:                    "character-inventory: all WearSet seats and fills are explicit");
14423:     scenario_check(footprints == 6 && bounded,
14424:                    "character-inventory: all spatial footprints remain bounded");
14425:     scenario_check(state.billboards.fk_panel_fill.ready() && ornate_panel &&
14426:                        life_orb && resource_orb && xp_rail &&
14427:                        skill_rasters == 4,
14428:                    "character-inventory: tiled panel fill, authored HUD, orb, rail, and skill rasters were drawn");
14429:   }
14430:   for (const auto& trace : state.hud_rect_trace) {
14431:     if (trace.first != "pane-item-footprint") continue;
14432:     state.mouse.x = trace.second.x + trace.second.w / 2;
14433:     state.mouse.y = trace.second.y + trace.second.h / 2;
14434:     break;
14435:   }
14436:   reference_present(state, 1366, 768, "");
14437:   scenario_check(state.selected_item == 0,
14438:                  "character-inventory: footprint hover selects the exact item");
14439:   move_inventory_selection(state, 1, 0);
14440:   scenario_check(state.selected_item == 1,
14441:                  "character-inventory: arrow navigation follows spatial neighbors");
14442:   return 0;
14443: }

14752:       sling_line = true;
14753:     if (item.op == render::Op::PaneStat &&
14754:         item.label.find("PEN 50%") != std::string::npos)
14755:       penetration_totals = true;
14756:     if (item.op == render::Op::Damage && item.label == "piercing:range")
14757:       piercing_number = true;
14758:   }
14759:   int forge_rows_inside = 0;
14760:   for (const auto& trace : state.hud_rect_trace)
14761:     if (trace.first == "pane-progression" ||
14762:         trace.first == "pane-progression-detail") {
14763:       inside = inside && trace.second.x >= expected.x &&
14764:                trace.second.x + trace.second.w <= expected.x + expected.w;
14765:       ++forge_rows_inside;
14766:     }
14767:   scenario_check(sling_line && penetration_totals && piercing_number,
14768:                  "vesselforge-implicits: Sling line, worn total, and PIERCE beat agree");
14769:   scenario_check(inside && forge_rows_inside == 2,
14770:                  "vesselforge-implicits: two-row forge totals remain inside Framekit");
14771:   return 0;
14772: }

14774: class ScenarioLineageSession final

14830:     model_.chronicle.active_house_id.clear();

14842: int scenario_chronicles_lineage_ui() {
14843:   ClientState state;
14844:   auto owned = std::make_unique<ScenarioLineageSession>();
14845:   ScenarioLineageSession* scenario = owned.get();
14846:   state.session = std::move(owned);
14847:   state.chronicles_mode = true;
14848:   state.screen = Screen::Chronicles;
14849:   state.chronicles_selected = 1;
14850:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
14851: 
14852:   std::string capture_dir;
14853:   const int capture_override = capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
14854:   if (capture_override < 0) {
14855:     scenario_check(false,
14856:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: capture root rejected before any write");
14857:     return 0;
14858:   }
14859:   if (capture_override == 0) {
14860:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
14861:     capture_dir = "captures";
14862:   }
14863: 
14864:   for (const auto [width, height] :
14865:        {std::pair<int, int>{960, 600}, {1366, 768}}) {
14866:     const std::string capture =
14867:         capture_dir + "\\chronicles-lineage-" + std::to_string(width) + "x" +
14868:         std::to_string(height) + ".png";
14869:     scenario_check(reference_present(state, width, height, capture),
14870:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: responsive Framekit capture written");
14871:     std::printf("    capture: %s\n", capture.c_str());
14872: 
14873:     const HudRect* house_pane = nullptr;
14874:     const HudRect* action_pane = nullptr;
14875:     const HudRect* title = nullptr;
14876:     const HudRect* subtitle = nullptr;
14877:     for (const auto& trace : state.hud_rect_trace) {
14878:       if (trace.first == "chronicles-house-pane") house_pane = &trace.second;
14879:       if (trace.first == "chronicles-action-pane") action_pane = &trace.second;
14880:       if (trace.first == "chronicles-title") title = &trace.second;
14881:       if (trace.first == "chronicles-subtitle") subtitle = &trace.second;
14882:     }
14883:     scenario_check(house_pane && action_pane &&
14884:                        !hud_rects_overlap(*house_pane, *action_pane),
14885:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: ledger and admission rail stay distinct");
14886:     scenario_check(title && subtitle &&
14887:                        subtitle->y >= title->y + title->h + 6 * hud_scale(height),
14888:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: masthead and subtitle keep a readable gap");
14889:     scenario_check(state.chronicles_action_hits.size() == 4,
14890:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: two Scions, creation, and oath are actionable");
14891:     bool bounded = true;
14892:     for (const auto& hit : state.chronicles_action_hits)
14893:       bounded = bounded && hit.rect.left >= 0 && hit.rect.top >= 0 &&
14894:                 hit.rect.right <= width && hit.rect.bottom <= height;
14895:     scenario_check(bounded,
14896:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: every action remains inside the viewport");
14897:     scenario_check(render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14898:                                    "house Emberwake") &&
14899:                        render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14900:                                        "scion scion-ilyra") &&
14901:                        render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14902:                                        "crypt crypt-sael") &&
14903:                        render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14904:                                        "mastery:23/64"),
14905:                    "chronicles-lineage-ui: House, living, remembered, and Wayfinder state render");
14906:     scenario_check(
14907:         state.billboards.framekit_status == "art: WIZARD Framekit 15/15" &&
14908:             render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14909:                             "framekit-raster:chronicle-laurel") &&
14910:             render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14911:                             "framekit-raster:chronicle-knocker"),
14912:         "chronicles-lineage-ui: WIZARD laurel and House knocker draw at runtime");
14913:   }
14914: 
14915: 
14916:   state.chronicles_naming = ChronicleNamingMode::Scion;
14917:   const std::string naming_capture =
14918:       capture_dir + "\\chronicles-lineage-naming-1366x768.png";
14919:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 1366, 768, naming_capture),
14920:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: raster naming ceremony captured");
14921:   scenario_check(render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14922:                                  "framekit-raster:chronicle-input"),
14923:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: WIZARD input field draws under live text");
14924:   for (const auto mode : {ChronicleNamingMode::House, ChronicleNamingMode::Scion}) {
14925:     state.chronicles_naming = mode;
14926:     state.chronicles_name_input = "WWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWWW";
14927:     for (const auto size : {std::pair{960,600}, std::pair{1727,1395}}) {
14928:       const std::string filename = capture_dir + "\\naming-long-" +
14929:           (mode == ChronicleNamingMode::House ? "house-" : "scion-") +
14930:           std::to_string(size.first) + "x" + std::to_string(size.second) + ".png";
14931:       scenario_check(reference_present(state,size.first,size.second,filename),
14932:                      "chronicles-lineage-ui: long name captured at owner and small-window sizes");
14933:       scenario_check(state.chronicles_name_input.size() == 28,
14934:                      "chronicles-lineage-ui: scrolling does not truncate the actual name");
14935:       scenario_check(render_list_has(state,render::Op::Chronicles,"naming:value:") &&
14936:                      !render_list_has(state,render::Op::Chronicles,
14937:                                       "naming:value:" + state.chronicles_name_input + "|"),
14938:                      "chronicles-lineage-ui: overflowing name scrolls inside authored frame");
14939:     }
14940:   }
14941:   state.chronicles_name_input.clear();
14942:   state.chronicles_naming = ChronicleNamingMode::None;
14943: 
14944:   state.chronicles_selected = 0;
14945:   handle_chronicles_key(state, VK_DOWN);
14946:   scenario_check(state.chronicles_selected == 1,
14947:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: arrow keys move the Scion selection");
14948:   scenario_present(state);
14949:   const auto oath_hit = state.chronicles_action_hits.back();
14950:   scenario_check(activate_chronicle_at(
14951:                      state, (oath_hit.rect.left + oath_hit.rect.right) / 2,
14952:                      (oath_hit.rect.top + oath_hit.rect.bottom) / 2) &&
14953:                      state.chronicles_oath,
14954:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: pointer arms the same mortal-oath action");
14955:   scenario_present(state);
14956:   const auto scion_hit = state.chronicles_action_hits.front();
14957:   scenario_check(activate_chronicle_at(
14958:                      state, (scion_hit.rect.left + scion_hit.rect.right) / 2,
14959:                      (scion_hit.rect.top + scion_hit.rect.bottom) / 2) &&
14960:                      scenario->submitted &&
14961:                      scenario->last_command.type ==
14962:                          verdigris::client::ClientCommand::Type::SelectScion &&
14963:                      scenario->last_command.target == "scion-ilyra" &&
14964:                      scenario->last_command.value == 1,
14965:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: pointer admission uses the oath-bearing server command");
14966:   scenario->add_scion_pages();
14967:   state.chronicles_selected = 10;
14968:   scenario_present(state);
14969:   scenario_check(state.chronicles_menu.size() == 11 &&
14970:                      state.chronicles_action_hits.size() <
14971:                          state.chronicles_menu.size() &&
14972:                      !state.chronicles_action_hits.empty() &&
14973:                      state.chronicles_action_hits.back().index == 10,
14974:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: a large lineage pages around the selected action");
14975:   scenario->clear_houses();
14976:   state.chronicles_selected = 0;
14977:   const std::string unfounded_capture =
14978:       capture_dir + "\\chronicles-unfounded-960x600.png";
14979:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, unfounded_capture),
14980:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: unfounded House lock captured");
14981:   scenario_check(render_list_has(state, render::Op::Chronicles,
14982:                                  "framekit-raster:chronicle-keyhole"),
14983:                  "chronicles-lineage-ui: an unfounded House shows the WIZARD keyhole");
14984:   return 0;
14985: }

14987: int scenario_guided_creation() {
14988:   using namespace verdigris::client;
14989:   std::unique_ptr<verdigris::networking::WebSocketServer> server;
14990:   unsigned short port=0;
14991:   for (unsigned short p=6780;p<=6799;++p) {
14992:     auto candidate=std::make_unique<verdigris::networking::WebSocketServer>(p);
14993:     std::string error;
14994:     if (candidate->start(&error)) { server=std::move(candidate); port=p; break; }
14995:   }
14996:   scenario_check(server!=nullptr,"creation: isolated real server starts");
14997:   if (!server) return scenario_failures;
14998:   ClientState state;
14999:   state.title_enabled=state.title_open=state.chronicles_mode=true;
15000:   state.screen=Screen::Chronicles;
15001:   state.session=std::make_unique<RemoteProtocolSession>("127.0.0.1",port,"guided-creation-proof",false);
15002:   std::string error;
15003:   scenario_check(state.session->start(&error),"creation: real remote session connects");
15004:   scenario_check(chronicles_pump(state,250,[&]{return title_account_ready(state);}),"creation: saved roster arrives before admission");
15005:   activate_title_action(state,0);
15006:   scenario_check(state.startup_creation && state.chronicles_naming==ChronicleNamingMode::House,"creation: first action asks only for House name");
15007:   activate_creation_control(state,2);
15008:   scenario_check(state.creation_wait==ClientState::CreationWait::None && !state.chronicles_name_error.empty(),"creation: blank name cannot silently create a default House");
15009:   scenario_type_chronicle_name(state,"The Copper Lantern");
15010:   activate_creation_control(state,2);
15011:   activate_creation_control(state,2); // double-click must not emit another founder
15012:   scenario_check(chronicles_pump(state,250,[&]{return state.chronicles_naming==ChronicleNamingMode::Scion;}),"creation: saved House leads directly to Scion naming, without a ledger detour");
15013:   scenario_check(state.session->model().chronicle.houses.size()==1,"creation: repeated confirm does not duplicate the House");
15014:   scenario_type_chronicle_name(state,"Ilyra Copper-Hand");
15015:   scenario_check(state.chronicles_name_input=="Ilyra Copper-Hand","creation: next-step first character is not swallowed");
15016:   activate_creation_control(state,1);
15017:   scenario_check(state.chronicles_oath,"creation: one control arms Hardcore");
15018:   std::string capture_dir;
15019:   const int override_state=capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
15020:   if (override_state<0) { state.session->shutdown(); server->stop(); scenario_check(false,"creation: invalid capture root"); return scenario_failures; }
15021:   if (!override_state) capture_dir="native/build/creation-captures";
15022:   std::filesystem::create_directories(capture_dir);
15023:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
15024:   for (POINT size : {POINT{960,600},POINT{1727,1395},POINT{3440,1440}}) {
15025:     reference_present(state,size.x,size.y,capture_dir+"/creation-hardcore-"+std::to_string(size.x)+"x"+std::to_string(size.y)+".png");
15026:     scenario_check(render_list_has(state,render::Op::Chronicles,"creation:hardcore:on"),"creation: Hardcore choice is visible on the name card");
15027:     scenario_check(!render_list_has(state,render::Op::Chronicles,"action:"),"creation: legacy ledger actions do not crowd guided creation");
15028:     scenario_check(state.creation_back.left>=0 && state.creation_confirm.right<=size.x && state.creation_confirm.bottom<=size.y,"creation: buttons fit resized windows");
15029:   }
15030:   activate_creation_control(state,2);
15031:   activate_creation_control(state,2);
15032:   scenario_check(chronicles_pump(state,250,[&]{return !state.startup_creation && state.screen==Screen::Expedition;}),"creation: exact saved Scion is admitted automatically");
15033:   const auto& model=state.session->model();
15034:   const auto* scion=find_chronicle_scion(model.chronicle,model.chronicle.active_scion_id);
15035:   scenario_check(scion && scion->name=="Ilyra Copper-Hand" && scion->mortal,"creation: server roster preserves chosen name and Hardcore oath");
15036:   scenario_check(model.chronicle.houses.size()==1 && model.chronicle.houses.front().scions.size()==1,"creation: repeated confirmation creates only one character");
15037:   // Return to title only in the fixture; production never teleports the live player here.
15038:   state.title_open=true;
15039:   activate_title_action(state,1);
15040:   scenario_check(!state.chronicles_oath,"creation: a separate new Scion does not inherit the prior oath selection");
15041:   scenario_type_chronicle_name(state,"Second Copper");
15042:   activate_creation_control(state,2);
15043:   cancel_startup_creation(state); // cancel only presentation, never undo a saved request
15044:   scenario_check(chronicles_pump(state,250,[&]{return state.session->model().chronicle.houses.front().scions.size()==2;}),"creation: late save receipt remains in the roster after Back");
15045:   scenario_check(state.title_open && !state.startup_creation,"creation: a late receipt cannot reopen a canceled wizard or auto-admit");
15046:   const auto* second=find_chronicle_scion(state.session->model().chronicle,state.session->model().chronicle.created_scion_id);
15047:   scenario_check(second && second->name=="Second Copper" && !second->mortal,"creation: unchecked Hardcore is stored as a soft character");
15048:   state.session->shutdown(); server->stop();
15049:   return scenario_failures;
15050: }

15052: int scenario_title_island() {
15053:   ClientState state;
15054:   auto session = std::make_unique<ScenarioLineageSession>();
15055:   auto* recorder = session.get();
15056:   state.session = std::move(session);
15057:   state.title_open = state.title_enabled = state.chronicles_mode = true;
15058:   state.screen = Screen::Chronicles;
15059:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
15060:   recorder->connection = verdigris::client::ConnectionState::Connected;
15061:   scenario_check(title_account_ready(state), "title: saved roster enables menu before world admission");
15062:   recorder->connection = verdigris::client::ConnectionState::Retrying;
15063:   scenario_check(!activate_title_action(state,0) && state.title_open && !recorder->submitted,
15064:                  "title: reconnect cannot submit stale admissions");
15065:   recorder->connection = verdigris::client::ConnectionState::Connected;
15066:   scenario_check(activate_title_action(state,0) && recorder->submitted &&
15067:                  recorder->last_command.type == verdigris::client::ClientCommand::Type::SelectScion &&
15068:                  recorder->last_command.target == "scion-ilyra" && recorder->last_command.value == 0,
15069:                  "title: continue keeps the living Scion identity and soft oath");
15070:   state.title_open = true;
15071:   scenario_check(activate_title_action(state,1) && state.chronicles_naming == ChronicleNamingMode::Scion,
15072:                  "title: create opens real Scion naming");
15073:   append_chronicle_text(state,L"O'Rin\r\n-Copper",14);
15074:   scenario_check(state.chronicles_name_input == "O'Rin-Copper" && state.chronicles_naming == ChronicleNamingMode::Scion,
15075:                  "naming: paste accepts a name but cannot submit embedded newlines");
15076:   append_chronicle_text(state,L" Long Unbounded Clipboard Payload",33);
15077:   scenario_check(state.chronicles_name_input.size()==28 && !state.chronicles_name_error.empty(),
15078:                  "naming: clipboard insertion obeys the same length cap as typing");
15079:   cancel_chronicle_naming(state);
15080:   recorder->clear_houses();
15081:   state.title_open = true;
15082:   scenario_check(activate_title_action(state,0) && state.chronicles_naming == ChronicleNamingMode::House,
15083:                  "title: first-time begin opens House founding");
15084:   cancel_chronicle_naming(state);
15085:   state.title_open = true;
15086:   scenario_check(activate_title_action(state,2) && !state.title_open && state.screen==Screen::Chronicles,
15087:                  "title: secondary menu opens the lineage ledger");
15088:   state.title_open = true;
15089:   std::string capture_dir;
15090:   const int override_state = capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
15091:   if (override_state < 0) { scenario_check(false,"title: invalid capture root"); return scenario_failures; }
15092:   if (!override_state) capture_dir = "native/build/title-captures";
15093:   std::filesystem::create_directories(capture_dir);
15094:   for (POINT size : {POINT{960,600},POINT{1727,1395},POINT{3440,1440}}) {
15095:     scenario_check(reference_present(state,size.x,size.y,capture_dir+"/title-"+std::to_string(size.x)+"x"+std::to_string(size.y)+".png"),
15096:                    "title: responsive native scene captured");
15097:     bool inside=state.title_action_hits.size()==3;
15098:     for (RECT hit : state.title_action_hits)
15099:       inside=inside && hit.left>=0 && hit.top>=0 && hit.right<=size.x && hit.bottom<=size.y;
15100:     scenario_check(inside,"title: all menu targets stay inside the resized window");
15101:     scenario_check(state.title_scene.error().empty() && state.title_scene.triangles()==119259,
15102:                    "title: actual WIZARD mesh rendered, not a fallback image");
15103:     if (!state.title_scene.error().empty()) std::printf("    title graphics: %s\n",state.title_scene.error().c_str());
15104:   }
15105:   constexpr int width=3440,height=1440;
15106:   BITMAPINFO info{}; info.bmiHeader.biSize=sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER); info.bmiHeader.biWidth=width;
15107:   info.bmiHeader.biHeight=-height; info.bmiHeader.biPlanes=1; info.bmiHeader.biBitCount=32;
15108:   void* bits=nullptr; HDC dc=CreateCompatibleDC(nullptr);
15109:   HBITMAP bitmap=CreateDIBSection(dc,&info,DIB_RGB_COLORS,&bits,nullptr,0);
15110:   if (!bitmap) { scenario_check(false,"title: frame surface allocation"); DeleteDC(dc); return scenario_failures; }
15111:   const auto old=SelectObject(dc,bitmap); RECT bounds{0,0,width,height};
15112:   paint_scene(state,dc,bounds);
15113:   auto fingerprint=[&]() {
15114:     std::uint64_t value=1469598103934665603ULL;
15115:     auto* bytes=static_cast<unsigned char*>(bits);
15116:     for(size_t i=0;i<static_cast<size_t>(width)*height*4;i+=127) value=(value^bytes[i])*1099511628211ULL;
15117:     return value;
15118:   };
15119:   const auto before=fingerprint();
15120:   state.title_orbit.previous={0,0}; state.title_orbit.dragging=true; state.title_orbit.drag({200,40}); state.title_orbit.dragging=false;
15121:   paint_scene(state,dc,bounds);
15122:   scenario_check(before!=fingerprint(),"title: orbit changes actual rendered geometry pixels");
15123:   for(int i=0;i<100;++i) state.title_orbit.wheel(WHEEL_DELTA);
15124:   scenario_check(state.title_orbit.zoom==.78f,"title: close zoom clamps safely");
15125:   for(int i=0;i<100;++i) state.title_orbit.wheel(-WHEEL_DELTA);
15126:   scenario_check(state.title_orbit.zoom==1.35f,"title: far zoom clamps safely");
15127:   state.title_orbit.reset();
15128:   scenario_check(state.title_orbit.yaw==0 && state.title_orbit.pitch==0 && state.title_orbit.zoom==1 && !state.title_orbit.dragging,
15129:                  "title: Home restores the authored view");
15130:   const auto begin=std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
15131:   for(int i=0;i<20;++i) paint_scene(state,dc,bounds);
15132:   const double ms=std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-begin).count()/20;
15133:   std::printf("    title frame-budget: %.1f ms, 20 real 3440x1440 frames\n",ms);
15134:   scenario_check(ms<40,"title: interactive native composition stays below 40ms");
15135:   verdigris::client::TitleScene missing;
15136:   scenario_check(!missing.draw(dc,bounds,"native/build/nonexistent-title-assets",state.title_orbit,0) && !missing.error().empty(),
15137:                  "title: missing assets produce an explicit error, not fake success");
15138:   SelectObject(dc,old); DeleteObject(bitmap); DeleteDC(dc);
15139:   return scenario_failures;
15140: }

15466: int scenario_town_social_hub() {
15467:   ClientState state;
15468:   state.session = std::make_unique<ScenarioDialogueSession>();
15469:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
15470:   sync_world(state);
15471:   generate_scenery(state);
15472:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
15473: 
15474:   std::string capture_dir;
15475:   const int capture_override = capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
15476:   if (capture_override < 0) {
15477:     scenario_check(false,
15478:                    "town-social-hub: capture root rejected before any write");
15479:     return 0;
15480:   }
15481:   if (capture_override == 0) {
15482:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
15483:     capture_dir = "captures";
15484:   }
15485:   const std::string capture_path =
15486:       capture_dir + "\\town-social-hub-1366x768.png";
15487:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 1366, 768, capture_path),
15488:                  "town-social-hub: Framekit dialogue evidence captured");
15489:   std::printf("    capture: %s\n", capture_path.c_str());
15490: 
15491:   bool pane = false;
15492:   int options = 0;
15493:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
15494:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "dialogue-pane") pane = true;
15495:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
15496:         item.label.rfind("dialogue-option:", 0) == 0)
15497:       ++options;
15498:   }
15499:   scenario_check(pane, "town-social-hub: authoritative dialogue pane is visible");
15500:   scenario_check(options == 3 && state.trade_row_hits.size() == 3,
15501:                  "town-social-hub: bank and both founding choices are actionable");
15502:   bool inside = true;
15503:   for (const auto& hit : state.trade_row_hits)
15504:     inside = inside && hit.rect.left >= 0 && hit.rect.top >= 0 &&
15505:              hit.rect.right <= 1366 && hit.rect.bottom <= 768;
15506:   scenario_check(inside,
15507:                  "town-social-hub: every choice remains inside the compact viewport");
15508:   return 0;
15509: }
15510: 
15511: int scenario_town_vesselforge() {
15512:   ClientState state;
15513:   auto owned = std::make_unique<ScenarioForgeSession>();
15514:   ScenarioForgeSession* scenario = owned.get();
15515:   state.session = std::move(owned);
15516:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
15517:   sync_world(state);
15518:   generate_scenery(state);
15519:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
15520: 
15521:   std::string capture_dir;
15522:   const int capture_override = capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
15523:   if (capture_override < 0) {
15524:     scenario_check(false,
15525:                    "town-vesselforge: capture root rejected before any write");
15526:     return 0;
15527:   }
15528:   if (capture_override == 0) {
15529:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
15530:     capture_dir = "captures";
15531:   }
15532:   const std::string compact =
15533:       capture_dir + "\\town-vesselforge-960x600.png";
15534:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, compact),
15535:                  "town-vesselforge: compact Framekit service captured");
15536:   std::printf("    capture: %s\n", compact.c_str());
15537:   bool compact_inside = state.trade_row_hits.size() == 6;
15538:   for (const auto& hit : state.trade_row_hits)
15539:     compact_inside = compact_inside && hit.rect.left >= 0 && hit.rect.top >= 0 &&
15540:                      hit.rect.right <= 960 && hit.rect.bottom <= 600;
15541:   scenario_check(compact_inside,
15542:                  "town-vesselforge: five-row page stays inside 960x600");
15543: 
15544:   state.trade_selected = 3;
15545:   const std::string large =
15546:       capture_dir + "\\town-vesselforge-1366x768.png";
15547:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 1366, 768, large),
15548:                  "town-vesselforge: selected vessel detail captured");
15549:   std::printf("    capture: %s\n", large.c_str());
15550:   bool pane = false;
15551:   bool active_line = false;
15552:   bool memory = false;
15553:   bool dormant_bond = false;
15554:   bool trophy_action = false;
15555:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
15556:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "vesselforge-pane") pane = true;
15557:     if (item.op == render::Op::PaneStat &&
15558:         item.label == "vesselforge-line:Ignores half of Armour")
15559:       active_line = true;
15560:     if (item.op == render::Op::PaneStat &&
15561:         item.label.rfind("vesselforge-memory:ATTUNEMENT  42/135", 0) == 0)
15562:       memory = true;
15563:     if (item.op == render::Op::PaneStat &&
15564:         item.label.rfind("vesselforge-line:Dormant - BOND:", 0) == 0)
15565:       dormant_bond = true;
15566:     if (item.op == render::Op::PaneStat &&
15567:         item.label == "vesselforge-trophy:boar_tusk")
15568:       trophy_action = true;
15569:   }
15570:   scenario_check(pane && active_line && memory && dormant_bond &&
15571:                      trophy_action && state.trade_row_hits.size() == 6,
15572:                  "town-vesselforge: Framekit list, progress, Bond, trophy, and paging evidence agree");
15573:   for (const auto& hit : state.trade_row_hits) {
15574:     if (hit.index == state.trade_selected) {
15575:       activate_trade_row(state, hit);
15576:       break;
15577:     }
15578:   }
15579:   scenario_check(
15580:       scenario->last_type == verdigris::client::ClientCommand::Type::MenuAction &&
15581:           scenario->last_target == "player:vesselforge:add-brand" &&
15582:           scenario->last_extra == "vf-sling" && scenario->last_value == 100,
15583:       "town-vesselforge: Enter/click routes the exact authoritative item and cost");
15584:   for (const auto& hit : state.trade_row_hits) {
15585:     if (hit.kind == 6 && hit.index == state.trade_selected) {
15586:       activate_trade_row(state, hit);
15587:       break;
15588:     }
15589:   }
15590:   scenario_check(
15591:       scenario->last_type == verdigris::client::ClientCommand::Type::MenuAction &&
15592:           scenario->last_target == "player:vesselforge:socket-trophy" &&
15593:           scenario->last_extra == "vf-sling" &&
15594:           scenario->last_auxiliary == "boar_tusk",
15595:       "town-vesselforge: T/click routes the exact vessel and trophy identities");
15596:   return 0;
15597: }

15599: int scenario_campaign_journal() {
15600:   ClientState state;
15601:   state.session = std::make_unique<ScenarioCampaignSession>();
15602:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
15603:   sync_world(state);
15604:   generate_scenery(state);
15605:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
15606:   state.gear_overlay = true;
15607:   state.character_pane = true;
15608:   state.tree_pane = true;
15609:   toggle_quest_journal(state);
15610:   scenario_check(state.quest_journal && !state.gear_overlay &&
15611:                      !state.character_pane && !state.tree_pane,
15612:                  "campaign-journal: opening dismisses narrower character panes");
15613: 
15614:   std::string capture_dir;
15615:   const int capture_override = capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
15616:   if (capture_override < 0) {
15617:     scenario_check(false,
15618:                    "campaign-journal: capture root rejected before any write");
15619:     return 0;
15620:   }
15621:   if (capture_override == 0) {
15622:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
15623:     capture_dir = "captures";
15624:   }
15625:   const std::string capture_path =
15626:       capture_dir + "\\campaign-journal-1366x768.png";
15627:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 1366, 768, capture_path),
15628:                  "campaign-journal: Framekit campaign evidence captured");
15629:   std::printf("    capture: %s\n", capture_path.c_str());
15630: 
15631:   bool pane = false;
15632:   bool active = false;
15633:   bool objective = false;
15634:   int completed = 0;
15635:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
15636:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "quest-journal") pane = true;
15637:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
15638:         item.label == "quest-active:oath-of-tin") active = true;
15639:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
15640:         item.label ==
15641:             "quest-objective:Break its Warden and open the road onward.")
15642:       objective = true;
15643:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
15644:         item.label.rfind("quest-complete:", 0) == 0)
15645:       ++completed;
15646:   }
15647:   scenario_check(pane && active && objective && completed == 4,
15648:                  "campaign-journal: active rite and completed deeds use authoritative copy");
15649:   const HudRect expected = quest_journal_rect(1366, 768);
15650:   const HudRect* footer = nullptr;
15651:   for (const auto& entry : state.hud_rect_trace)
15652:     if (entry.first == "quest-pane-footer") footer = &entry.second;
15653:   scenario_check(expected.x >= 0 && expected.y >= 0 &&
15654:                      expected.x + expected.w <= 1366 &&
15655:                      expected.y + expected.h <= 768 && footer &&
15656:                      footer->x + footer->w <= expected.x + expected.w,
15657:                  "campaign-journal: journal and controls fit the compact viewport");
15658:   state.quit_requested = false;
15659:   handle_escape_key(state);
15660:   scenario_check(!state.quest_journal && !state.quit_requested,
15661:                  "campaign-journal: Escape closes journal before exiting");
15662:   return 0;
15663: }

15867: int scenario_tactical_map_overlay() {
15868:   ClientState state;
15869:   scenario_begin(state);
15870:   load_billboards(state.billboards);
15871:   state.simulation.reset();  // hold the authored navigation fixture still
15872: 
15873:   constexpr int kWidth = 1366;
15874:   constexpr int kHeight = 768;
15875:   constexpr int kMapSize = 64;
15876:   state.world.player.position = {32 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits),
15877:                                  32 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits)};
15878:   state.world.player.facing = {1, -1};
15879:   state.world.player.life = 94;
15880:   state.world.player.life_max = 110;
15881:   state.world.player.resource = 37;
15882:   state.world.player.resource_max = 50;
15883:   state.world.house_name = "House Emberwake";
15884:   state.world.scion_name = "Ilyra";
15885:   state.world.route_id = "route:copper:3:2";
15886:   state.world.theme = "crypt";
15887:   state.world.map_width = kMapSize;
15888:   state.world.map_height = kMapSize;
15889:   state.world.map_walkable.assign(kMapSize * kMapSize, 0);
15890:   const auto open = [&](int x, int y) {
15891:     if (x >= 0 && x < kMapSize && y >= 0 && y < kMapSize)
15892:       state.world.map_walkable[static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * kMapSize + x] = 1;
15893:   };
15894:   // Crossroads, side chambers, and a loop produce recognizable topology
15895:   // rather than a capture-only rectangle.
15896:   for (int x = 18; x <= 48; ++x)
15897:     for (int y = 30; y <= 34; ++y) open(x, y);
15898:   for (int y = 14; y <= 50; ++y)
15899:     for (int x = 30; x <= 34; ++x) open(x, y);
15900:   for (int x = 20; x <= 27; ++x)
15901:     for (int y = 22; y <= 27; ++y) open(x, y);
15902:   for (int x = 39; x <= 46; ++x)
15903:     for (int y = 38; y <= 45; ++y) open(x, y);
15904:   for (int x = 23; x <= 43; ++x) {
15905:     open(x, 18);
15906:     open(x, 19);
15907:     open(x, 47);
15908:     open(x, 48);
15909:   }
15910:   for (int y = 18; y <= 48; ++y) {
15911:     open(23, y);
15912:     open(24, y);
15913:     open(42, y);
15914:     open(43, y);
15915:   }
15916:   state.world.has_extraction = true;
15917:   state.world.extraction = {43 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits),
15918:                             43 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits)};
15919:   state.world.monsters.clear();
15920:   WorldActor foe;
15921:   foe.id = "map-foe";
15922:   foe.name = "Ossuary Wight";
15923:   foe.position = {36 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits),
15924:                   32 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits)};
15925:   foe.life = foe.life_max = 80;
15926:   state.world.monsters.push_back(foe);
15927:   WorldActor elite = foe;
15928:   elite.id = "map-elite";
15929:   elite.name = "Sepulchral Keeper";
15930:   elite.position = {42 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits),
15931:                     39 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits)};
15932:   elite.elite = true;
15933:   state.world.monsters.push_back(elite);
15934:   state.scenery = {
15935:       {SceneryKind::Shrine,
15936:        {24 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits),
15937:         24 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits)},
15938:        kTileUnits * 0.3, 1.0, false},
15939:       {SceneryKind::Ruin,
15940:        {32 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits),
15941:         18 * static_cast<int>(kTileUnits)},
15942:        kTileUnits * 0.3, 1.0, false},
15943:   };
15944: 
15945:   state.gear_overlay = true;
15946:   state.character_pane = true;
15947:   state.tree_pane = true;
15948:   toggle_minimap_overlay(state);
15949:   scenario_check(state.minimap_mode == MinimapMode::Overlay &&
15950:                      !state.gear_overlay && !state.character_pane &&
15951:                      !state.tree_pane,
15952:                  "tactical-map: opening the broad chart dismisses narrower panes");
15953: 
15954:   const int zoom_before = state.minimap_zoom_step;
15955:   adjust_minimap_zoom(state, 1);
15956:   scenario_check(state.minimap_zoom_step == zoom_before + 1,
15957:                  "tactical-map: zoom setting advances through production control");
15958:   adjust_minimap_zoom(state, -1);
15959:   const int opacity_before = state.minimap_opacity_step;
15960:   adjust_minimap_opacity(state, -1);
15961:   scenario_check(state.minimap_opacity_step == opacity_before - 1,
15962:                  "tactical-map: opacity setting advances through production control");
15963:   adjust_minimap_opacity(state, 1);
15964:   for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) adjust_minimap_zoom(state, 1);
15965:   scenario_check(state.minimap_zoom_step == kMinimapSettingSteps - 1,
15966:                  "tactical-map: zoom clamps at the supported maximum");
15967:   state.minimap_zoom_step = kDefaultMinimapZoomStep;
15968:   state.hint.clear();
15969:   state.hint_ticks = 0;
15970: 
15971:   std::string capture_dir;
15972:   const int capture_override = capture_root_override(&capture_dir);
15973:   if (capture_override < 0) {
15974:     scenario_check(false,
15975:                    "tactical-map: capture root rejected before any write");
15976:     return 0;
15977:   }
15978:   if (capture_override == 0) {
15979:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
15980:     capture_dir = "captures";
15981:   }
15982:   const std::string settings_path =
15983:       capture_dir + "\\tactical-map-preferences.ini";
15984:   state.minimap_side = MinimapSide::Right;
15985:   state.minimap_zoom_step = 3;
15986:   state.minimap_opacity_step = 1;
15987:   const bool settings_written =
15988:       write_minimap_preferences(state, settings_path);
15989:   ClientState restored;
15990:   const bool settings_read =
15991:       read_minimap_preferences(restored, settings_path);
15992:   scenario_check(settings_written && settings_read &&
15993:                      restored.minimap_side == MinimapSide::Right &&
15994:                      restored.minimap_zoom_step == 3 &&
15995:                      restored.minimap_opacity_step == 1,
15996:                  "tactical-map: side, zoom, and opacity survive a client restart");
15997:   (void)DeleteFileA(settings_path.c_str());
15998:   state.minimap_side = MinimapSide::Left;
15999:   state.minimap_zoom_step = kDefaultMinimapZoomStep;
16000:   state.minimap_opacity_step = kDefaultMinimapOpacityStep;
16001:   const std::string capture_path =
16002:       capture_dir + "\\tactical-map-overlay-1366x768.png";
16003:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, kWidth, kHeight, capture_path),
16004:                  "tactical-map: translucent Framekit overlay captured");
16005:   std::printf("    capture: %s\n", capture_path.c_str());
16006: 
16007:   const render::Item* overlay_op = nullptr;
16008:   bool controls = false;
16009:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
16010:     if (item.op == render::Op::Minimap &&
16011:         item.label.rfind("overlay:", 0) == 0)
16012:       overlay_op = &item;
16013:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("map-control:", 0) == 0)
16014:       controls = true;
16015:   }
16016:   scenario_check(overlay_op &&
16017:                      overlay_op->label.find(":cells=0") == std::string::npos,
16018:                  "tactical-map: overlay renders authoritative walkability topology");
16019:   scenario_check(controls,
16020:                  "tactical-map: zoom, opacity, side, and close controls are discoverable");
16021:   const HudRect expected = minimap_overlay_rect(kWidth, kHeight);
16022:   scenario_check(expected.x >= 0 && expected.y >= 0 &&
16023:                      expected.x + expected.w <= kWidth &&
16024:                      expected.y + expected.h <= kHeight - 96,
16025:                  "tactical-map: overlay clears the viewport and combat HUD");
16026: 
16027:   toggle_minimap_overlay(state);
16028:   swap_minimap_side(state);
16029:   reference_present(state, kWidth, kHeight, "");
16030:   const render::Item* corner = render::first(state.render_list, render::Op::Minimap);
16031:   scenario_check(corner && corner->x > kWidth / 2 &&
16032:                      corner->label.rfind("corner:right", 0) == 0,
16033:                  "tactical-map: compact map honors right-side placement");
16034: 
16035:   toggle_minimap_overlay(state);
16036:   state.quit_requested = false;
16037:   handle_escape_key(state);
16038:   scenario_check(state.minimap_mode == MinimapMode::Corner &&
16039:                      !state.quit_requested,
16040:                  "tactical-map: Escape closes the chart before requesting exit");
16041:   return 0;

16043: std::string art_wave_capture_dir();
16044: 
16045: int scenario_effect_batch() {
16046:   ClientState state;
16047:   scenario_begin(state);
16048:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16049:   const verdigris::Vec2 origin = state.world.player.position;
16050:   auto& cache = gdi_object_cache();
16051:   cache.pen_hits = 0;
16052:   cache.pen_misses = 0;
16053:   constexpr int kBurst = 40;
16054:   for (int i = 0; i < kBurst; ++i) {
16055:     EffectFx impact;
16056:     impact.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Impact;
16057:     impact.wx = static_cast<double>(origin.x + i * 3);
16058:     impact.wy = static_cast<double>(origin.y);
16059:     impact.ttl = 8;
16060:     add_effect(state, impact);
16061:     EffectFx swing;
16062:     swing.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Swing;
16063:     swing.wx = static_cast<double>(origin.x);
16064:     swing.wy = static_cast<double>(origin.y + i * 3);
16065:     swing.ttl = 6;
16066:     add_effect(state, swing);
16067:   }
16068:   ActiveTelegraph warning;
16069:   warning.actor_id = "batch-warn";
16070:   warning.action = "thrust";
16071:   warning.position = origin;
16072:   warning.facing = {1, 0};
16073:   warning.start_tick = state.world.tick;
16074:   warning.windup_ticks = 12;
16075:   state.telegraphs["batch-warn"] = warning;
16076:   scenario_present(state);
16077:   const int first_hits = cache.pen_hits;
16078:   const int first_misses = cache.pen_misses;
16079:   int impacts = 0;
16080:   int swings = 0;
16081:   int monsters = 0;
16082:   bool warning_drawn = false;
16083:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
16084:     if (item.op == render::Op::Impact) ++impacts;
16085:     if (item.op == render::Op::Swing) ++swings;
16086:     if (item.op == render::Op::Monster) ++monsters;
16087:     if (item.op == render::Op::Telegraph) warning_drawn = true;
16088:   }
16089:   scenario_check(impacts >= kBurst,
16090:                  "effect-batch: every impact sprite still records an Impact op");
16091:   scenario_check(swings >= kBurst,
16092:                  "effect-batch: every swing arc still records a Swing op");
16093:   scenario_check(warning_drawn,
16094:                  "effect-batch: dropping the telegraph cannot pass the batch");
16095:   scenario_check(monsters > 0,
16096:                  "effect-batch: actors remain on the painter after the batch");
16097:   std::printf("    effect-batch first paint: world %.1f ms | pen hits %d misses %d\n",
16098:               state.paint_ms_world, first_hits, first_misses);
16099:   scenario_present(state);
16100:   std::printf("    effect-batch reuse paint: world %.1f ms | pen hits %d misses %d\n",
16101:               state.paint_ms_world, cache.pen_hits, cache.pen_misses);
16102:   scenario_check(cache.pen_hits > first_hits,
16103:                  "effect-batch: second pass reuses pens instead of CreatePen");
16104:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Telegraph),
16105:                  "effect-batch: warning still present on the reuse pass");
16106:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16107:   if (dir.empty()) {
16108:     scenario_check(false, "effect-batch: capture root rejected before any write");
16109:     return scenario_failures;
16110:   }
16111:   const std::string report = dir + "\\effect-batch-report.txt";
16112:   FILE* out = nullptr;
16113:   fopen_s(&out, report.c_str(), "w");
16114:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "effect-batch: report opened");
16115:   if (out) {
16116:     std::fprintf(out, "pen_hits=%d\npen_misses=%d\nimpacts=%d\nswings=%d\n",
16117:                  cache.pen_hits, cache.pen_misses, impacts, swings);
16118:     std::fclose(out);
16119:   }
16120:   const std::string png = dir + "\\effect-batch-960x600.png";
16121:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16122:                  "effect-batch: reused-pen capture written");
16123:   return scenario_failures;
16124: }

16126: int scenario_hitch_warmup() {
16127:   reset_gdi_object_cache();
16128:   ClientState cold;
16129:   scenario_begin(cold);
16130:   scenario_follow_camera(cold);
16131:   scenario_present(cold);
16132:   seed_combat_hitch_fx(cold);
16133:   scenario_present(cold);
16134:   const double cold_ms = cold.last_paint_ms;
16135:   bool cold_swing = render::any(cold.render_list, render::Op::Swing);
16136:   bool cold_damage = render::any(cold.render_list, render::Op::Damage);
16137:   scenario_present(cold);
16138:   const double warm_ms = cold.last_paint_ms;
16139: 
16140:   reset_gdi_object_cache();
16141:   ClientState prepared;
16142:   scenario_begin(prepared);
16143:   scenario_follow_camera(prepared);
16144:   scenario_present(prepared);
16145:   warm_combat_glyphs();
16146:   seed_combat_hitch_fx(prepared);
16147:   scenario_present(prepared);
16148:   const double prepared_ms = prepared.last_paint_ms;
16149:   std::printf("    hitch-warmup cold combat paint: %.1f ms (must be reported)\n",
16150:               cold_ms);
16151:   std::printf("    hitch-warmup warm combat paint: %.1f ms\n", warm_ms);
16152:   std::printf("    hitch-warmup prepared combat paint: %.1f ms "
16153:               "(glyphs warmed before the strike)\n",
16154:               prepared_ms);
16155:   scenario_check(cold_ms > 0.0,
16156:                  "hitch-warmup: hiding the cold trace cannot pass");
16157:   scenario_check(warm_ms > 0.0, "hitch-warmup: warm trace is required");
16158:   scenario_check(prepared_ms > 0.0, "hitch-warmup: prepared trace is required");
16159:   scenario_check(cold_swing && cold_damage,
16160:                  "hitch-warmup: cold combat still draws swing and damage");
16161:   scenario_check(render::any(prepared.render_list, render::Op::Swing) &&
16162:                      render::any(prepared.render_list, render::Op::Damage),
16163:                  "hitch-warmup: warmup cannot drop required combat ops");
16164:   scenario_check(prepared_ms <= cold_ms + 8.0,
16165:                  "hitch-warmup: prepared strike is not slower than the cold hit");
16166:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16167:   if (dir.empty()) {
16168:     scenario_check(false, "hitch-warmup: capture root rejected before any write");
16169:     return scenario_failures;
16170:   }
16171:   const std::string report = dir + "\\hitch-warmup-report.txt";
16172:   FILE* out = nullptr;
16173:   fopen_s(&out, report.c_str(), "w");
16174:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "hitch-warmup: report opened");
16175:   if (out) {
16176:     std::fprintf(out, "cold_ms=%.3f\nwarm_ms=%.3f\nprepared_ms=%.3f\n", cold_ms,
16177:                  warm_ms, prepared_ms);
16178:     std::fclose(out);
16179:   }
16180:   const std::string png = dir + "\\hitch-warmup-960x600.png";
16181:   scenario_check(reference_present(prepared, 960, 600, png),
16182:                  "hitch-warmup: prepared-strike capture written");
16183:   return scenario_failures;
16184: }

16186: int scenario_attack_poses() {
16187:   ClientState state;
16188:   scenario_begin(state);
16189:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16190:   scenario_present(state);
16191:   auto has_pose = [&](const char* name) {
16192:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16193:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
16194:     return false;
16195:   };
16196:   scenario_check(has_pose("attack-pose:idle"),
16197:                  "attack-poses: standing Scion is labeled idle");
16198:   scenario_check(!has_pose("attack-pose:active"),
16199:                  "attack-poses: idle cannot wear the active strike pose");
16200: 
16201:   const verdigris::Vec2 origin = state.world.player.position;
16202:   EffectFx swing;
16203:   swing.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Swing;
16204:   swing.wx = static_cast<double>(origin.x);
16205:   swing.wy = static_cast<double>(origin.y);
16206:   swing.ttl = 6;
16207:   swing.age = 0;
16208:   add_effect(state, swing);
16209:   scenario_present(state);
16210:   scenario_check(has_pose("attack-pose:windup"),
16211:                  "attack-poses: windup is a distinct cocking pose");
16212:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Swing),
16213:                  "attack-poses: windup still draws the swing arc");
16214: 
16215:   for (auto& fx : state.effects)
16216:     if (fx.kind == EffectFx::Kind::Swing) fx.age = 3;
16217:   scenario_present(state);
16218:   scenario_check(has_pose("attack-pose:active"),
16219:                  "attack-poses: active is a distinct committed pose");
16220: 
16221:   for (auto& fx : state.effects)
16222:     if (fx.kind == EffectFx::Kind::Swing) fx.age = 5;
16223:   scenario_present(state);
16224:   scenario_check(has_pose("attack-pose:recovery"),
16225:                  "attack-poses: recovery is a distinct settle pose");
16226: 
16227:   EffectFx dust;
16228:   dust.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Dust;
16229:   dust.wx = static_cast<double>(origin.x);
16230:   dust.wy = static_cast<double>(origin.y);
16231:   dust.angle = 0.2;
16232:   dust.ttl = 8;
16233:   add_effect(state, dust);
16234:   scenario_present(state);
16235:   scenario_check(has_pose("attack-pose:cancel"),
16236:                  "attack-poses: dash dust during a swing is a cancel pose");
16237:   scenario_check(vector_art::idle_as_attack_family_fails_review(true),
16238:                  "attack-poses: idle alone cannot certify the strike family");
16239:   scenario_check(!vector_art::idle_as_attack_family_fails_review(false),
16240:                  "attack-poses: distinct stages are not the idle anti-pattern");
16241:   scenario_check(vector_art::stick_attack_fails_review(false, true, true),
16242:                  "attack-poses: a strike without a cocked windup cannot pass");
16243:   scenario_check(!vector_art::stick_attack_fails_review(
16244:                      vector_art::kAttackWindupCocksBlade,
16245:                      vector_art::kAttackActiveExtendsBlade, true),
16246:                  "attack-poses: windup cocks and active extends a readable blade");
16247: 
16248:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
16249:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
16250:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
16251:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
16252:   if (state.simulation && !state.simulation->ground_items().empty())
16253:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::pick_up(
16254:                              state.simulation->ground_items().front().id));
16255:   if (state.simulation && !state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty())
16256:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::equip(
16257:                              state.simulation->scion().carried_items.front().id));
16258:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16259:   state.effects.erase(std::remove_if(state.effects.begin(), state.effects.end(),
16260:                                      [](const EffectFx& fx) {
16261:                                        return fx.kind == EffectFx::Kind::Dust;
16262:                                      }),
16263:                       state.effects.end());
16264:   bool have_swing = false;
16265:   for (auto& fx : state.effects) {
16266:     if (fx.kind != EffectFx::Kind::Swing && fx.kind != EffectFx::Kind::SweepArc)
16267:       continue;
16268:     fx.age = std::max(1, fx.ttl / 2);
16269:     have_swing = true;
16270:   }
16271:   if (!have_swing) {
16272:     EffectFx live;
16273:     live.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Swing;
16274:     live.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x);
16275:     live.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
16276:     live.ttl = 6;
16277:     live.age = 3;
16278:     add_effect(state, live);
16279:   }
16280:   state.pose_review_strip = true;
16281:   scenario_present(state);
16282:   scenario_check(has_pose("attack-pose:active"),
16283:                  "attack-poses: capture pose is the committed active strike");
16284:   bool strip = false;
16285:   bool strip_windup = false;
16286:   bool strip_active = false;
16287:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
16288:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
16289:     if (item.label == "pose-strip") strip = true;
16290:     if (item.label == "pose-strip:windup") strip_windup = true;
16291:     if (item.label == "pose-strip:active") strip_active = true;
16292:   }
16293:   scenario_check(strip && strip_windup && strip_active,
16294:                  "attack-poses: native strip paints windup and active, not labels only");
16295:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16296:   if (dir.empty()) {
16297:     scenario_check(false, "attack-poses: capture root rejected before any write");
16298:     return scenario_failures;
16299:   }
16300:   const std::string png = dir + "\\attack-poses-960x600.png";
16301:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16302:                  "attack-poses: windup/active/cancel family capture written");
16303:   return scenario_failures;
16304: }
16305: 
16306: std::string art_wave_capture_dir() {
16307:   std::string forced;
16308:   const int overridden = capture_root_override(&forced);
16309:   if (overridden != 0) return overridden > 0 ? forced : std::string{};
16310:   const std::string repo = repository_root_for_capture_validation();
16311:   if (repo.empty()) {
16312:     CreateDirectoryA("captures", nullptr);
16313:     return "captures";
16314:   }
16315:   const std::string dir = repo + "\\docs\\execution\\captures\\art-wave";
16316:   create_directories_nested(dir);
16317:   return dir;
16318: }

16320: int scenario_kit_chunk() {
16321:   ClientState state;
16322:   scenario_begin(state);
16323:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16324:   scenario_present(state);
16325:   bool kinds[5] = {};
16326:   int solid_items = 0;
16327:   int proxy_ops = 0;
16328:   int scenery_ops = 0;
16329:   int gate_proxy = 0;
16330:   double tree_radius_lo = 1e9;
16331:   double tree_radius_hi = 0.0;
16332:   for (const auto& item : state.scenery) {
16333:     const int kind = static_cast<int>(item.kind);
16334:     if (kind >= 0 && kind < 5) kinds[kind] = true;
16335:     if (item.solid) ++solid_items;
16336:   }
16337:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
16338:     if (item.op == render::Op::Scenery) {
16339:       ++scenery_ops;
16340:       if (item.label == "tree") {
16341:         tree_radius_lo = std::min(tree_radius_lo, item.radius);
16342:         tree_radius_hi = std::max(tree_radius_hi, item.radius);
16343:       }
16344:     }
16345:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
16346:         item.label.rfind("collision-proxy:", 0) == 0) {
16347:       ++proxy_ops;
16348:       if (item.label == "collision-proxy:gate") ++gate_proxy;
16349:     }
16350:   }
16351:   scenario_check(kinds[0] && kinds[1] && kinds[2] && kinds[3] && kinds[4],
16352:                  "kit-chunk: village kit includes tree, ruin, dwelling, shrine, gate");
16353:   scenario_check(scenery_ops >= 10,
16354:                  "kit-chunk: kit parts are in the production render list");
16355:   scenario_check(proxy_ops == solid_items,
16356:                  "kit-chunk: every solid piece publishes a collision proxy");
16357:   scenario_check(gate_proxy == 0,
16358:                  "kit-chunk: the dressing gate is not an unreported obstacle");
16359:   scenario_check(tree_radius_hi > tree_radius_lo + 0.5,
16360:                  "kit-chunk: scaled trees keep distinct proxy radii");
16361:   scenario_check(vector_art::lollipop_tree_fails_review(1, false, false),
16362:                  "kit-chunk: a circle-on-stick is the lollipop negative");
16363:   scenario_check(!vector_art::lollipop_tree_fails_review(
16364:                      vector_art::kTreeCanopyClusters, vector_art::kTreeHasRootFlare,
16365:                      vector_art::kTreeHasFork),
16366:                  "kit-chunk: the shipped tree is not a lollipop");
16367:   scenario_check(vector_art::stall_dwelling_fails_review(false, false, true),
16368:                  "kit-chunk: a scalloped stall cannot certify a dwelling");
16369:   scenario_check(!vector_art::stall_dwelling_fails_review(
16370:                      vector_art::kDwellingHasWalls, vector_art::kDwellingHasRoof,
16371:                      vector_art::kDwellingHasDoor),
16372:                  "kit-chunk: dwellings have walls, thatch, and a door");
16373:   scenario_check(vector_art::wagon_ruin_fails_review(false, false, true),
16374:                  "kit-chunk: a covered wagon cannot certify a ruin");
16375:   scenario_check(!vector_art::wagon_ruin_fails_review(
16376:                      vector_art::kRuinHasBrokenWall, vector_art::kRuinHasRubble,
16377:                      vector_art::kRuinHasWheels),
16378:                  "kit-chunk: ruins have a broken wall and rubble, not wheels");
16379:   scenario_check(vector_art::blob_shrine_fails_review(false, false, false),
16380:                  "kit-chunk: a stone blob cannot certify a shrine");
16381:   scenario_check(!vector_art::blob_shrine_fails_review(
16382:                      vector_art::kShrineHasBasin, vector_art::kShrineHasColumn,
16383:                      vector_art::kShrineHasWater),
16384:                  "kit-chunk: shrines have a basin, column, and water");
16385:   scenario_check(vector_art::slab_gate_fails_review(false, false, false),
16386:                  "kit-chunk: a solid slab cannot certify a gate");
16387:   scenario_check(!vector_art::slab_gate_fails_review(
16388:                      vector_art::kGateHasPillars, vector_art::kGateHasLintel,
16389:                      vector_art::kGateHasOpening),
16390:                  "kit-chunk: gates have pillars, a lintel, and an opening");
16391: 
16392:   {
16393:     const RECT kit_bounds{0, 0, 960, 600};
16394:     bool shrine_on_screen = false;
16395:     bool gate_on_screen = false;
16396:     for (const auto& item : state.scenery) {
16397:       const ScreenPoint p =
16398:           project(state.camera, kit_bounds, item.position.x, item.position.y);
16399:       if (p.x < 48 || p.x >= 912 || p.y < 48 || p.y >= 552) continue;
16400:       if (item.kind == SceneryKind::Shrine) shrine_on_screen = true;
16401:       if (item.kind == SceneryKind::Gate) gate_on_screen = true;
16402:     }
16403:     scenario_check(shrine_on_screen,
16404:                    "kit-chunk: the shrine is inside the spawn capture");
16405:     scenario_check(gate_on_screen,
16406:                    "kit-chunk: the dressing gate is inside the spawn capture");
16407:   }
16408: 
16409:   std::unordered_set<std::string> occupancy;
16410:   bool unique_landmarks = true;
16411:   for (const auto& item : state.scenery) {
16412:     char key[64];
16413:     std::snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "%d,%d,%d", item.position.x, item.position.y,
16414:                   static_cast<int>(item.kind));
16415:     if (!occupancy.insert(key).second) unique_landmarks = false;
16416:   }
16417:   scenario_check(unique_landmarks,
16418:                  "kit-chunk: kit pieces do not stack on one pivot");
16419: 
16420:   sync_world(state);
16421:   const verdigris::Vec2 spawn = state.world.player.position;
16422:   bool spawn_clear = true;
16423:   for (const auto& item : state.scenery) {
16424:     if (!item.solid) continue;
16425:     const double dx = static_cast<double>(item.position.x - spawn.x);
16426:     const double dy = static_cast<double>(item.position.y - spawn.y);
16427:     const double min = item.radius + kActorColliderRadius;
16428:     if (dx * dx + dy * dy < min * min) spawn_clear = false;
16429:   }
16430:   scenario_check(spawn_clear,
16431:                  "kit-chunk: spawn is outside published solid radii");
16432: 
16433:   verdigris::Vec2 from = spawn;
16434:   verdigris::Vec2 into_tree = spawn;
16435:   bool found_tree = false;
16436:   for (const auto& item : state.scenery) {
16437:     if (item.kind == SceneryKind::Tree && item.solid) {
16438:       into_tree = item.position;
16439:       found_tree = true;
16440:       break;
16441:     }
16442:   }
16443:   scenario_check(found_tree && scenery_blocks_segment(state, from, into_tree),
16444:                  "kit-chunk: collision uses the same solids as the render proxies");
16445: 
16446:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16447:   if (dir.empty()) {
16448:     scenario_check(false, "kit-chunk: capture root rejected before any write");
16449:     return scenario_failures;
16450:   }
16451:   const std::string png = dir + "\\kit-chunk-960x600.png";
16452:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16453:                  "kit-chunk: village kit capture written");
16454:   return scenario_failures;
16455: }
16456: 
16457: int scenario_weave_vfx() {
16458:   ClientState state;
16459:   scenario_begin(state);
16460:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16461:   const verdigris::Vec2 origin = state.world.player.position;
16462:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
16463:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16464:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
16465:     return false;
16466:   };
16467:   auto warcry_radius = [&]() {
16468:     double r = 0.0;
16469:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16470:       if (item.op == render::Op::WarCry && item.radius > r) r = item.radius;
16471:     return r;
16472:   };
16473: 
16474:   EffectFx aura;
16475:   aura.kind = EffectFx::Kind::WarCryAura;
16476:   aura.wx = static_cast<double>(origin.x);
16477:   aura.wy = static_cast<double>(origin.y);
16478:   aura.ttl = 14;
16479:   aura.age = 0;
16480:   add_effect(state, aura);
16481:   scenario_present(state);
16482:   scenario_check(has_hud("vfx-weave:cast"),
16483:                  "weave-vfx: apply beat is labeled cast");
16484:   scenario_check(warcry_radius() > 2.0,
16485:                  "weave-vfx: cast still draws a WarCry ring");
16486: 
16487:   for (auto& fx : state.effects)
16488:     if (fx.kind == EffectFx::Kind::WarCryAura) fx.age = 6;
16489:   scenario_present(state);
16490:   scenario_check(has_hud("vfx-weave:travel"),
16491:                  "weave-vfx: mid lifetime is the travel beat");
16492: 
16493:   for (auto& fx : state.effects)
16494:     if (fx.kind == EffectFx::Kind::WarCryAura) fx.age = 10;
16495:   scenario_present(state);
16496:   scenario_check(has_hud("vfx-weave:impact"),
16497:                  "weave-vfx: late lifetime is the impact beat");
16498:   const double impact_r = warcry_radius();
16499:   const double cap = std::min(960.0, 600.0) / 6.0;
16500:   scenario_check(impact_r <= cap,
16501:                  "weave-vfx: impact radius stays inside the screen sixth cap");
16502:   scenario_check(impact_r < 600.0 * 0.45,
16503:                  "weave-vfx: a large screen-filling ring cannot pass");
16504: 
16505:   state.effects.clear();
16506:   EffectFx fade;
16507:   fade.kind = EffectFx::Kind::WarCryFade;
16508:   fade.wx = static_cast<double>(origin.x);
16509:   fade.wy = static_cast<double>(origin.y);
16510:   fade.ttl = 10;
16511:   fade.age = 0;
16512:   add_effect(state, fade);
16513:   scenario_present(state);
16514:   scenario_check(has_hud("vfx-weave:cancel"),
16515:                  "weave-vfx: fade is the cancellation beat");
16516:   bool fade_label = false;
16517:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16518:     if (item.op == render::Op::WarCry && item.label == phase_a::kWarcryFadeLabel)
16519:       fade_label = true;
16520:   scenario_check(fade_label, "weave-vfx: cancel keeps the TASK-0122 fade label");
16521: 
16522:   state.effects.clear();
16523:   const auto* before = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
16524:   scenario_check(before != nullptr, "weave-vfx: scion exists for telegraph overlap");
16525:   if (before) {
16526:     const int melee = verdigris::world_scale::kMeleeRange;
16527:     state.simulation->spawn_monster(
16528:         {before->position.x - melee, before->position.y}, 1, true);
16529:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Wait));
16530:     scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Telegraph),
16531:                    "weave-vfx: enemy warning is present before the weave");
16532:     EffectFx overlay;
16533:     overlay.kind = EffectFx::Kind::WarCryAura;
16534:     overlay.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x);
16535:     overlay.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
16536:     overlay.ttl = 14;
16537:     overlay.age = 10;
16538:     add_effect(state, overlay);
16539:     scenario_present(state);
16540:     scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Telegraph),
16541:                    "weave-vfx: spectacle cannot hide the telegraph");
16542:     scenario_check(has_hud("vfx-weave:impact"),
16543:                    "weave-vfx: weave still records while the warning is up");
16544:     scenario_check(warcry_radius() <= cap,
16545:                    "weave-vfx: overlapping weave still respects the radius cap");
16546:   }
16547: 
16548:   scenario_check(vector_art::blob_weave_fails_review(false, true, true, true),
16549:                  "weave-vfx: a blob without cast motes cannot certify the family");
16550:   scenario_check(!vector_art::blob_weave_fails_review(
16551:                      vector_art::kWeaveHasCastMotes, vector_art::kWeaveHasTravelOrbit,
16552:                      vector_art::kWeaveHasImpactTicks,
16553:                      vector_art::kWeaveHasCancelImplode),
16554:                  "weave-vfx: cast/travel/impact/cancel share bronze identity");
16555: 
16556:   for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i)
16557:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
16558:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16559:   state.effects.clear();
16560:   EffectFx shown;
16561:   shown.kind = EffectFx::Kind::WarCryAura;
16562:   shown.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x);
16563:   shown.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
16564:   shown.ttl = 14;
16565:   shown.age = 6;
16566:   add_effect(state, shown);
16567:   state.weave_review_strip = true;
16568:   scenario_present(state);
16569:   bool strip = false;
16570:   bool strip_cast = false;
16571:   bool strip_travel = false;
16572:   bool strip_impact = false;
16573:   bool strip_cancel = false;
16574:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
16575:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
16576:     if (item.label == "weave-strip") strip = true;
16577:     if (item.label == "weave-strip:cast") strip_cast = true;
16578:     if (item.label == "weave-strip:travel") strip_travel = true;
16579:     if (item.label == "weave-strip:impact") strip_impact = true;
16580:     if (item.label == "weave-strip:cancel") strip_cancel = true;
16581:   }
16582:   scenario_check(strip && strip_cast && strip_travel && strip_impact &&
16583:                      strip_cancel,
16584:                  "weave-vfx: native strip paints all four weave beats");
16585:   scenario_check(has_hud("vfx-weave:travel"),
16586:                  "weave-vfx: capture pose is the travel beat at game scale");
16587: 
16588:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16589:   if (dir.empty()) {
16590:     scenario_check(false, "weave-vfx: capture root rejected before any write");
16591:     return scenario_failures;
16592:   }
16593:   const std::string png = dir + "\\weave-vfx-960x600.png";
16594:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16595:                  "weave-vfx: cast/impact family capture written");
16596:   return scenario_failures;
16597: }
