16599: int scenario_pad_path() {
16600:   ClientState state;
16601:   scenario_begin(state);
16602:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16603:   state.mouse.x = 480;
16604:   state.mouse.y = 300;
16605:   scenario_present(state);
16606:   auto has = [&](const char* name) {
16607:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16608:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
16609:     return false;
16610:   };
16611:   scenario_check(!has("pad:connected"),
16612:                  "pad-path: mouse position does not count as a controller");
16613:   scenario_check(!has("pad-glyph:A"),
16614:                  "pad-path: mouse emulation cannot mint pad glyphs");
16615: 
16616:   state.pad.inject = true;
16617:   state.pad.connected = true;
16618:   poll_pad(state);
16619:   scenario_present(state);
16620:   scenario_check(has("pad:connected") && has("pad-glyph:LS") && has("pad-glyph:A") &&
16621:                      has("pad-glyph:B") && has("pad-glyph:X") && has("pad-glyph:Y"),
16622:                  "pad-path: connected pad publishes live glyphs");
16623:   scenario_check(has("pad:hotplug:in"),
16624:                  "pad-path: hotplug-in is recorded");
16625: 
16626:   const verdigris::Vec2 before = state.world.player.position;
16627:   state.pad.dx = 1;
16628:   RECT bounds{0, 0, 960, 600};
16629:   for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
16630:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16631:   scenario_present(state);
16632:   scenario_check(state.world.player.position.x > before.x,
16633:                  "pad-path: left stick moves the Scion");
16634: 
16635:   for (int i = 0; i < 40; ++i) fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
16636:   state.pad.dx = 0;
16637:   state.pad.a = true;
16638:   fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
16639:   scenario_present(state);
16640:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Swing) ||
16641:                      render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Damage),
16642:                  "pad-path: A is strike, not a synthetic mouse click");
16643:   state.pad.a = false;
16644: 
16645:   scenario_check(!state.gear_overlay, "pad-path: inventory starts closed");
16646:   state.pad.y = true;
16647:   fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
16648:   scenario_check(state.gear_overlay, "pad-path: Y focuses the gear inventory");
16649:   state.pad.y = false;
16650:   fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
16651: 
16652:   state.pad.connected = false;
16653:   poll_pad(state);
16654:   scenario_present(state);
16655:   scenario_check(!has("pad:connected"),
16656:                  "pad-path: disconnect clears the connected glyph");
16657:   scenario_check(has("pad:hotplug:out"),
16658:                  "pad-path: hotplug-out is recorded");
16659: 
16660:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16661:   if (dir.empty()) {
16662:     scenario_check(false, "pad-path: capture root rejected before any write");
16663:     return scenario_failures;
16664:   }
16665:   state.pad.inject = true;
16666:   state.pad.connected = true;
16667:   poll_pad(state);
16668:   const std::string png = dir + "\\pad-path-960x600.png";
16669:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16670:                  "pad-path: controller HUD capture written");
16671:   return scenario_failures;
16672: }

16674: int scenario_legal_sounds() {
16675:   using verdigris::client::sound_family::shippable;
16676:   scenario_check(shippable("hit") && shippable("crit") && shippable("kill") &&
16677:                      shippable("scion-lost") && shippable("warcry-expire"),
16678:                  "legal-sounds: impact and warning cues carry SPDX provenance");
16679:   scenario_check(shippable("cosmetic"),
16680:                  "legal-sounds: swing-family placeholder is sourced");
16681:   scenario_check(!shippable("unlicensed-preview"),
16682:                  "legal-sounds: missing provenance cannot ship even if audible");
16683:   ClientState state;
16684:   scenario_begin(state);
16685:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
16686:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
16687:   for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
16688:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
16689:   bool heard = false;
16690:   bool all_legal = true;
16691:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced) {
16692:     if (cue.rfind("ambience:", 0) == 0 || cue.rfind("music:", 0) == 0) continue;
16693:     heard = true;
16694:     if (!shippable(cue.c_str())) {
16695:       all_legal = false;
16696:       std::printf("    illegal cue: %s\n", cue.c_str());
16697:     }
16698:   }
16699:   scenario_check(heard, "legal-sounds: an ordinary fight actually voiced cues");
16700:   scenario_check(all_legal,
16701:                  "legal-sounds: every voiced combat cue is in the licensed family");
16702:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16703:   if (dir.empty()) {
16704:     scenario_check(false, "legal-sounds: capture root rejected before any write");
16705:     return scenario_failures;
16706:   }
16707:   const std::string manifest = dir + "\\legal-sounds-provenance.txt";
16708:   FILE* out = nullptr;
16709:   fopen_s(&out, manifest.c_str(), "w");
16710:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "legal-sounds: provenance table opened");
16711:   if (out) {
16712:     std::fprintf(out, "family=combat\nnegative=unlicensed-preview\n");
16713:     for (const auto& row : verdigris::client::sound_family::kCombatFamily) {
16714:       std::fprintf(out, "%s %s %s %s\n", row.cue_id, row.role, row.license,
16715:                    row.source);
16716:     }
16717:     std::fclose(out);
16718:   }
16719:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16720:   const std::string png = dir + "\\legal-sounds-960x600.png";
16721:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16722:                  "legal-sounds: fight capture written");
16723:   return scenario_failures;
16724: }

16726: int scenario_music_phase() {
16727:   ClientState state;
16728:   scenario_begin(state);
16729:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16730:   drain_audio(state);
16731:   scenario_present(state);
16732:   auto has = [&](const char* name) {
16733:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16734:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
16735:     return false;
16736:   };
16737:   scenario_check(has("music:muted"),
16738:                  "music-phase: device mute is an explicit HUD state");
16739:   scenario_check(state.audio_music_want == "music:combat",
16740:                  "music-phase: slay-wardens with living foes wants combat");
16741:   scenario_check(has("music:phase:combat") && has("audio:mixer"),
16742:                  "music-phase: live HUD paints Theme Combat, not a mute chip alone");
16743:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::music::leftover_theme_fails_review(
16744:                      false, "music:combat"),
16745:                  "music-phase: a leftover combat send cannot certify unload");
16746:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::music::leftover_theme_fails_review(
16747:                      true, "music:combat"),
16748:                  "music-phase: a loaded combat theme is not treated as leftover");
16749:   bool heard_combat = false;
16750:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced)
16751:     if (cue == "music:combat") heard_combat = true;
16752:   scenario_check(heard_combat,
16753:                  "music-phase: combat theme is scheduled (device remains mute)");
16754: 
16755:   state.world.expedition_phase = ExpeditionPhaseView::ExtractCarriedValue;
16756:   state.world.monsters.clear();
16757:   drain_audio(state);
16758:   scenario_check(state.audio_music_want == "music:recovery",
16759:                  "music-phase: carry-to-exit is recovery music");
16760:   bool heard_recovery = false;
16761:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced)
16762:     if (cue == "music:recovery") heard_recovery = true;
16763:   scenario_check(heard_recovery, "music-phase: recovery theme is scheduled");
16764: 
16765:   state.simulation.reset();
16766:   state.session.reset();
16767:   drain_audio(state);
16768:   scenario_check(state.audio_music_want == "music:none",
16769:                  "music-phase: unloaded scene cannot leave a competing theme");
16770:   scenario_check(state.audio_music_sent == "music:none",
16771:                  "music-phase: paused/unloaded does not keep an active music send");
16772:   scenario_check(state.audio_mixer->bus_muted(verdigris::audio::Bus::Music),
16773:                  "music-phase: unload mutes the music bus");
16774:   bool leftover_music = false;
16775:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_mixer->drain_scheduled()) {
16776:     if (cue.cue_id.rfind("music:", 0) == 0) leftover_music = true;
16777:   }
16778:   scenario_check(!leftover_music && state.audio_mixer->pending().empty(),
16779:                  "music-phase: muted unload cannot voice a leftover combat loop");
16780: 
16781:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16782:   if (dir.empty()) {
16783:     scenario_check(false, "music-phase: capture root rejected before any write");
16784:     return scenario_failures;
16785:   }
16786:   ClientState capture;
16787:   scenario_begin(capture);
16788:   scenario_follow_camera(capture);
16789:   drain_audio(capture);
16790:   const std::string png = dir + "\\music-phase-960x600.png";
16791:   scenario_check(reference_present(capture, 960, 600, png),
16792:                  "music-phase: combat-theme capture written");
16793:   return scenario_failures;
16794: }

16796: int scenario_gpu_sample() {
16797:   verdigris::gpu::Sample sample;
16798:   scenario_check(!sample.init(static_cast<verdigris::gpu::Backend>(99)),
16799:                  "gpu-sample: an unknown backend cannot pretend to be portable");
16800:   scenario_check(sample.init(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software) && sample.alive,
16801:                  "gpu-sample: software backend opens a 64x64 window buffer");
16802:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(verdigris::gpu::Sample::kBackendName, "software") == 0,
16803:                  "gpu-sample: the decision is software, not a Windows-only device");
16804:   scenario_check(sample.draw_textured_quad(),
16805:                  "gpu-sample: textured quad draws without a GPU device");
16806:   const std::uint32_t inside = sample.pixel(32, 32);
16807:   const std::uint32_t outside = sample.pixel(2, 2);
16808:   scenario_check(inside == 0x00C48E40u || inside == 0x00767068u,
16809:                  "gpu-sample: quad samples the bronze/stone checker");
16810:   scenario_check(outside == 0x00182028u,
16811:                  "gpu-sample: clear color remains outside the quad");
16812:   ClientState hud;
16813:   scenario_begin(hud);
16814:   scenario_follow_camera(hud);
16815:   scenario_present(hud);
16816:   bool backend_hud = false;
16817:   for (const auto& item : hud.render_list)
16818:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "gpu-backend:software")
16819:       backend_hud = true;
16820:   scenario_check(backend_hud,
16821:                  "gpu-sample: live HUD names the software backend");
16822: 
16823:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16824:   if (dir.empty()) {
16825:     scenario_check(false, "gpu-sample: capture root rejected before any write");
16826:     sample.shutdown();
16827:     return scenario_failures;
16828:   }
16829:   const std::string bmp = dir + "\\gpu-sample-quad.bmp";
16830:   scenario_check(sample.write_bmp(bmp), "gpu-sample: portable BMP written");
16831:   sample.shutdown();
16832:   scenario_check(!sample.alive && sample.pixels.empty(),
16833:                  "gpu-sample: shutdown releases the window buffer");
16834:   scenario_check(!sample.draw_textured_quad(),
16835:                  "gpu-sample: drawing after shutdown fails closed");
16836:   return scenario_failures;
16837: }

16839: int scenario_gpu_packets() {
16840:   ClientState state;
16841:   scenario_begin(state);
16842:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16843:   const auto* before = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
16844:   scenario_check(before != nullptr, "gpu-packets: scion exists");
16845:   if (before) {
16846:     const int melee = verdigris::world_scale::kMeleeRange;
16847:     state.simulation->spawn_monster(
16848:         {before->position.x - melee, before->position.y}, 1, true);
16849:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Wait));
16850:   }
16851:   scenario_present(state);
16852:   const auto packets = verdigris::gpu::packets_from_render_list(state.render_list);
16853:   scenario_check(packets.size() == state.render_list.size(),
16854:                  "gpu-packets: every semantic op becomes a packet");
16855:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::snapshot_valid(packets),
16856:                  "gpu-packets: production packets carry no backend handles");
16857:   bool saw_telegraph = false;
16858:   for (const auto& packet : packets)
16859:     if (packet.op == static_cast<std::uint16_t>(render::Op::Telegraph))
16860:       saw_telegraph = true;
16861:   scenario_check(saw_telegraph,
16862:                  "gpu-packets: Telegraph is the adapted draw class");
16863:   const std::string snap = verdigris::gpu::snapshot_text(packets);
16864:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::snapshot_mentions_backend(snap),
16865:                  "gpu-packets: snapshot text has no HDC/D3D/pointer tokens");
16866:   scenario_present(state);
16867:   const std::string snap2 = verdigris::gpu::snapshot_text(
16868:       verdigris::gpu::packets_from_render_list(state.render_list));
16869:   scenario_check(snap == snap2,
16870:                  "gpu-packets: headless packets stay deterministic without GPU state");
16871: 
16872:   auto poisoned = packets;
16873:   if (!poisoned.empty()) poisoned[0].backend_handle = 0xD3D0001ull;
16874:   scenario_check(!poisoned.empty() && !verdigris::gpu::snapshot_valid(poisoned),
16875:                  "gpu-packets: a backend resource cannot enter a snapshot");
16876:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16877:   if (dir.empty()) {
16878:     scenario_check(false, "gpu-packets: capture root rejected before any write");
16879:     return scenario_failures;
16880:   }
16881:   const std::string snap_path = dir + "\\gpu-packets-snapshot.txt";
16882:   FILE* out = nullptr;
16883:   fopen_s(&out, snap_path.c_str(), "w");
16884:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "gpu-packets: snapshot file opened");
16885:   if (out) {
16886:     std::fprintf(out, "%s", snap.c_str());
16887:     std::fclose(out);
16888:   }
16889:   const std::string png = dir + "\\gpu-packets-960x600.png";
16890:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16891:                  "gpu-packets: Telegraph scene capture written");
16892:   return scenario_failures;
16893: }

16895: int scenario_visual_target() {
16896:   ClientState state;
16897:   scenario_begin(state);
16898:   // VG-ART-005: the composition sheet is a live expedition with a held
16899:   // weapon. A paper-doll seat or an unarmed crate cannot certify.
16900:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
16901:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
16902:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
16903:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
16904:   if (state.simulation && !state.simulation->ground_items().empty())
16905:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::pick_up(
16906:                              state.simulation->ground_items().front().id));
16907:   if (state.simulation && !state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty())
16908:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::equip(
16909:                              state.simulation->scion().carried_items.front().id));
16910:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
16911:   state.local_combat_xp = 36;
16912:   scenario_present(state);
16913:   auto has = [&](const char* name) {
16914:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16915:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
16916:     return false;
16917:   };
16918:   scenario_check(has("target:camera:top-down") && has("target:proportion:adult") &&
16919:                      has("target:palette:bronze-stone") &&
16920:                      has("target:contrast:ink-on-panel"),
16921:                  "visual-target: in-game composition axes are named");
16922:   scenario_check(!has("target:concept-art"),
16923:                  "visual-target: an external concept image cannot substitute");
16924:   scenario_check(vector_art::adult_head_passes(vector_art::kAdultHeadUnits,
16925:                                               vector_art::kAdultBodyUnits),
16926:                  "visual-target: the live rig uses an adult head ratio");
16927:   scenario_check(vector_art::chibi_head_fails_review(33.0, 100.0),
16928:                  "visual-target: a 1/3 head is the chibi negative");
16929:   scenario_check(!vector_art::chibi_head_fails_review(vector_art::kAdultHeadUnits,
16930:                                                      vector_art::kAdultBodyUnits),
16931:                  "visual-target: the shipped head cannot fail as chibi");
16932:   bool loader_chip = false;
16933:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
16934:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
16935:         item.label.find("PNG billboards loaded") != std::string::npos)
16936:       loader_chip = true;
16937:   scenario_check(!loader_chip,
16938:                  "visual-target: a loader chip cannot count as the composition sheet");
16939:   scenario_check(state.world.xp_fraction > 0.25 && state.world.xp_fraction < 0.85,
16940:                  "visual-target: the composition sheet shows a filled XP meter");
16941:   const render::Item* scion =
16942:       render::first(state.render_list, render::Op::Player);
16943:   scenario_check(scion && scion->label != "held:none",
16944:                  "visual-target: the composition sheet shows a held item");
16945:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::art::paper_doll_only_fails_review(
16946:                      true, scion && scion->label != "held:none"),
16947:                  "visual-target: a paper-doll seat alone cannot certify the sheet");
16948:   scenario_check(vector_art::lollipop_tree_fails_review(1, false, false),
16949:                  "visual-target: a circle-on-stick cannot certify the village kit");
16950:   scenario_check(!vector_art::lollipop_tree_fails_review(
16951:                      vector_art::kTreeCanopyClusters, vector_art::kTreeHasRootFlare,
16952:                      vector_art::kTreeHasFork),
16953:                  "visual-target: the live kit uses a forked clustered canopy");
16954:   scenario_check(vector_art::stall_dwelling_fails_review(false, false, true),
16955:                  "visual-target: a scalloped stall cannot certify a dwelling");
16956:   scenario_check(!vector_art::stall_dwelling_fails_review(
16957:                      vector_art::kDwellingHasWalls, vector_art::kDwellingHasRoof,
16958:                      vector_art::kDwellingHasDoor),
16959:                  "visual-target: dwellings have walls, thatch, and a door");
16960:   scenario_check(vector_art::wagon_ruin_fails_review(false, false, true),
16961:                  "visual-target: a covered wagon cannot certify a ruin");
16962:   scenario_check(!vector_art::wagon_ruin_fails_review(
16963:                      vector_art::kRuinHasBrokenWall, vector_art::kRuinHasRubble,
16964:                      vector_art::kRuinHasWheels),
16965:                  "visual-target: ruins have a broken wall and rubble, not wheels");
16966:   scenario_check(vector_art::blob_shrine_fails_review(false, false, false),
16967:                  "visual-target: a stone blob cannot certify a shrine");
16968:   scenario_check(!vector_art::blob_shrine_fails_review(
16969:                      vector_art::kShrineHasBasin, vector_art::kShrineHasColumn,
16970:                      vector_art::kShrineHasWater),
16971:                  "visual-target: shrines have a basin, column, and water");
16972:   scenario_check(vector_art::slab_gate_fails_review(false, false, false),
16973:                  "visual-target: a solid slab cannot certify a gate");
16974:   scenario_check(!vector_art::slab_gate_fails_review(
16975:                      vector_art::kGateHasPillars, vector_art::kGateHasLintel,
16976:                      vector_art::kGateHasOpening),
16977:                  "visual-target: gates have pillars, a lintel, and an opening");
16978:   scenario_check(vector_art::crate_foe_fails_review(false, false, false),
16979:                  "visual-target: a crate-shaped foe cannot certify the sheet");
16980:   scenario_check(!vector_art::crate_foe_fails_review(
16981:                      vector_art::kWardenHasJointedLegs, vector_art::kWardenHasSnout,
16982:                      vector_art::kWardenHasFilledClaws),
16983:                  "visual-target: town wardens use jointed legs, a snout, and claws");
16984:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
16985:   if (dir.empty()) {
16986:     scenario_check(false, "visual-target: capture root rejected before any write");
16987:     return scenario_failures;
16988:   }
16989:   const std::string png = dir + "\\visual-target-960x600.png";
16990:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
16991:                  "visual-target: in-game sheet capture written");
16992:   return scenario_failures;
16993: }

16995: int scenario_bronze_stone() {
16996:   using namespace verdigris::art::bronze_stone;
16997:   scenario_check(shippable() && !is_placeholder(kBronze) && !is_placeholder(kStone),
16998:                  "bronze-stone: family has provenance and is not a placeholder fill");
16999:   scenario_check(sample_albedo(0, 0) == kBronze && sample_albedo(7, 0) == kStone,
17000:                  "bronze-stone: albedo map has both metals");
17001:   scenario_check(sample_rim(0, 0) != sample_albedo(0, 0) ||
17002:                      sample_rim(7, 7) != sample_albedo(7, 7),
17003:                  "bronze-stone: rim map is a lighting response, not a copy of albedo");
17004:   ClientState state;
17005:   scenario_begin(state);
17006:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17007:   scenario_present(state);
17008:   bool stone = false;
17009:   bool family = false;
17010:   bool magenta = false;
17011:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17012:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "material:stone") stone = true;
17013:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "material:bronze-stone")
17014:       family = true;
17015:     if (item.label.find("FF00FF") != std::string::npos) magenta = true;
17016:   }
17017:   scenario_check(stone && family,
17018:                  "bronze-stone: village kit scenery samples the cooked family");
17019:   scenario_check(!magenta,
17020:                  "bronze-stone: magenta placeholder fills cannot pass");
17021:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17022:   if (dir.empty()) {
17023:     scenario_check(false, "bronze-stone: capture root rejected before any write");
17024:     return scenario_failures;
17025:   }
17026:   const std::string png = dir + "\\bronze-stone-960x600.png";
17027:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17028:                  "bronze-stone: cooked family capture written");
17029:   return scenario_failures;
17030: }

17032: int scenario_shader_bindings() {
17033:   verdigris::gpu::Bindings ok{};
17034:   verdigris::gpu::Bindings stale{};
17035:   verdigris::gpu::Bindings wrong{};
17036:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::load_bindings(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software,
17037:                                                verdigris::gpu::kBindingLayoutVersion,
17038:                                                &ok) &&
17039:                      ok.shader_id &&
17040:                      std::strcmp(ok.shader_id, verdigris::gpu::kSoftwareShaderId) == 0,
17041:                  "shader-bindings: software layout v1 loads without a source path");
17042:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::load_bindings(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software, 0,
17043:                                                 &stale) &&
17044:                      stale.albedo == nullptr,
17045:                  "shader-bindings: stale layout fails instead of rendering silently");
17046:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::load_bindings(
17047:                      static_cast<verdigris::gpu::Backend>(7),
17048:                      verdigris::gpu::kBindingLayoutVersion, &wrong) &&
17049:                      wrong.shader_id == nullptr,
17050:                  "shader-bindings: a D3D-only program cannot load on this sample");
17051:   verdigris::gpu::Sample sample;
17052:   sample.init(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software);
17053:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::draw_lit_quad(sample, stale),
17054:                  "shader-bindings: failed bindings do not draw a fallback fill");
17055:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::draw_lit_quad(sample, ok),
17056:                  "shader-bindings: cooked albedo/rim program draws the quad");
17057:   const std::uint32_t lit = sample.pixel(32, 32);
17058:   scenario_check(lit != 0x00182028u &&
17059:                      !verdigris::art::bronze_stone::is_placeholder(lit),
17060:                  "shader-bindings: lit texel is family-shaded, not placeholder");
17061:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17062:   if (dir.empty()) {
17063:     scenario_check(false, "shader-bindings: capture root rejected before any write");
17064:     sample.shutdown();
17065:     return scenario_failures;
17066:   }
17067:   scenario_check(sample.write_bmp(dir + "\\shader-bindings-quad.bmp"),
17068:                  "shader-bindings: cooked program capture written");
17069:   sample.shutdown();
17070:   return scenario_failures;
17071: }

17073: int scenario_gpu_reference() {
17074:   verdigris::gpu::Sample demo;
17075:   demo.init(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software);
17076:   demo.draw_textured_quad();
17077:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::present_reference_scene(demo, {}, 960, 600, false),
17078:                  "gpu-reference: a disconnected textured-quad demo cannot pass");
17079:   demo.shutdown();
17080: 
17081:   ClientState state;
17082:   scenario_begin(state);
17083:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17084:   const auto* scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
17085:   scenario_check(scion != nullptr, "gpu-reference: session has a scion");
17086:   if (scion) {
17087:     const int melee = verdigris::world_scale::kMeleeRange;
17088:     state.simulation->spawn_monster(
17089:         {scion->position.x - melee, scion->position.y}, 1, true);
17090:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Wait));
17091:   }
17092:   EffectFx hit;
17093:   hit.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Impact;
17094:   hit.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x);
17095:   hit.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
17096:   hit.ttl = 8;
17097:   add_effect(state, hit);
17098:   scenario_present_size(state, 960, 600);
17099:   const auto packets = verdigris::gpu::packets_from_render_list(state.render_list);
17100:   const auto census = verdigris::gpu::census_packets(packets);
17101:   scenario_check(census.actors > 0 && census.world > 0 && census.hud > 0 &&
17102:                      census.effects > 0 && census.target_sheet,
17103:                  "gpu-reference: session packets carry actors, world, effects, HUD");
17104:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::session_scene_complete(census),
17105:                  "gpu-reference: composition matches the in-game target sheet");
17106: 
17107:   verdigris::gpu::Sample sample;
17108:   scenario_check(sample.init(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software),
17109:                  "gpu-reference: software present opens on both platforms' sample");
17110:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::present_reference_scene(sample, packets, 960, 600,
17111:                                                          false),
17112:                  "gpu-reference: packets without a live session cannot present");
17113:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::present_reference_scene(sample, packets, 960, 600,
17114:                                                         true),
17115:                  "gpu-reference: live packets present through the GPU bridge");
17116:   const std::uint32_t lit = sample.pixel(sample.width / 2, sample.height / 2);
17117:   bool stamped = false;
17118:   for (int y = 0; y < sample.height && !stamped; ++y)
17119:     for (int x = 0; x < sample.width && !stamped; ++x)
17120:       if (sample.pixel(x, y) != 0x00182028u) stamped = true;
17121:   scenario_check(stamped && !verdigris::art::bronze_stone::is_placeholder(lit),
17122:                  "gpu-reference: session packets shaded the sample buffer");
17123:   (void)lit;
17124:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17125:   if (dir.empty()) {
17126:     scenario_check(false, "gpu-reference: capture root rejected before any write");
17127:     sample.shutdown();
17128:     return scenario_failures;
17129:   }
17130:   scenario_check(sample.write_bmp(dir + "\\gpu-reference-session.bmp"),
17131:                  "gpu-reference: session-connected BMP written");
17132:   sample.shutdown();
17133:   const std::string png = dir + "\\gpu-reference-960x600.png";
17134:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17135:                  "gpu-reference: live session capture written");
17136:   return scenario_failures;
17137: }
17138: 
17139: int scenario_grounding() {
17140:   ClientState state;
17141:   scenario_begin(state);
17142:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17143:   const auto* scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
17144:   scenario_check(scion != nullptr, "grounding: session has a scion");
17145:   if (scion) {
17146:     const int melee = verdigris::world_scale::kMeleeRange;
17147:     state.simulation->spawn_monster(
17148:         {scion->position.x - melee, scion->position.y}, 1, true);
17149:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Wait));
17150:   }
17151:   verdigris::Vec2 gate{200, 180};
17152:   bool found_gate = false;
17153:   for (const auto& item : state.scenery) {
17154:     if (item.kind != SceneryKind::Gate) continue;
17155:     gate = item.position;
17156:     found_gate = true;
17157:     break;
17158:   }
17159:   scenario_check(found_gate, "grounding: village gate is in the spawn frustum");
17160:   // Pin the sim-authored Sweep onto the gate so the capture proves a
17161:   // foreground wall cannot erase the warning. ingest_events drops keys
17162:   // that are not live actors, so this keeps the elite's id.
17163:   for (auto& entry : state.telegraphs) {
17164:     entry.second.position = gate;
17165:     entry.second.action = "sweep";
17166:     entry.second.reach = verdigris::Simulation::presentation_catalog().melee_range;
17167:     entry.second.windup_ticks = 8;
17168:     entry.second.start_tick =
17169:         state.world.tick > 5 ? state.world.tick - 5 : 0;
17170:   }
17171:   scenario_present(state);
17172:   auto has = [&](const char* name) {
17173:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17174:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
17175:     return false;
17176:   };
17177:   bool sweep_over_gate = false;
17178:   double sweep_px = 0.0;
17179:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17180:     if (item.op == render::Op::Telegraph && item.label == "sweep") {
17181:       sweep_over_gate = true;
17182:       sweep_px = std::max(sweep_px, item.radius);
17183:     }
17184:   }
17185:   scenario_check(has("grounding:sort:y") && has("grounding:contact-shadow"),
17186:                  "grounding: Y-sort and foot shadows are the named policy");
17187:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Telegraph),
17188:                  "grounding: a threat telegraph is in the production list");
17189:   scenario_check(sweep_over_gate && sweep_px >= 24.0,
17190:                  "grounding: Sweep paints a readable disc on the village gate");
17191:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::telegraph_draws_after_scenery(state.render_list),
17192:                  "grounding: a foreground wall cannot erase the telegraph pass");
17193:   scenario_check(has("grounding:telegraph-overlay") && has("grounding:over-scenery"),
17194:                  "grounding: the overlay pass is named");
17195:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::hud_label_alone_fails_grounding_review(false),
17196:                  "grounding: a HUD token without a telegraph cannot certify");
17197:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::hud_label_alone_fails_grounding_review(sweep_over_gate),
17198:                  "grounding: the painted Sweep, not a HUD token, certifies overlay");
17199:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::capture_black_telegraph_fails_review(0, 0, 0),
17200:                  "grounding: a capture-black fill cannot certify Sweep");
17201:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::capture_black_telegraph_fails_review(238, 72, 64),
17202:                  "grounding: red warning chroma is not treated as capture-black");
17203:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17204:   if (dir.empty()) {
17205:     scenario_check(false, "grounding: capture root rejected before any write");
17206:     return scenario_failures;
17207:   }
17208:   const std::string png = dir + "\\grounding-960x600.png";
17209:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17210:                  "grounding: telegraph-over-scenery capture written");
17211:   return scenario_failures;
17212: }
17213: 
17214: int scenario_material_light() {
17215:   verdigris::gpu::Bindings bind{};
17216:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::load_bindings(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software,
17217:                                                verdigris::gpu::kBindingLayoutVersion,
17218:                                                &bind),
17219:                  "material-light: bronze/stone bindings load");
17220:   const verdigris::gpu::Light a = verdigris::gpu::light_from_tick(0);
17221:   const verdigris::gpu::Light b = verdigris::gpu::light_from_tick(20);
17222:   const std::uint32_t pa = verdigris::gpu::shade_texel_lit(bind, 2, 2, a);
17223:   const std::uint32_t pb = verdigris::gpu::shade_texel_lit(bind, 2, 2, b);
17224:   scenario_check(pa != pb, "material-light: a moving light changes the material");
17225:   scenario_check(((pa >> 16) & 0xFF) <= verdigris::gpu::kLitChannelCap &&
17226:                      ((pb >> 16) & 0xFF) <= verdigris::gpu::kLitChannelCap,
17227:                  "material-light: lighting cannot wash the family to white");
17228:   verdigris::gpu::Sample sample;
17229:   sample.init(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software);
17230:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::draw_lit_quad_moving(sample, bind, a, true),
17231:                  "material-light: damage zone composites over the lit quad");
17232:   const std::uint32_t zone = sample.pixel(32, 32);
17233:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::damage_zone_concealed(zone),
17234:                  "material-light: overbright additives cannot conceal damage zones");
17235:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::washed_light_fails_review(0x00FFFFFFu),
17236:                  "material-light: a white wash cannot certify the family");
17237:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::washed_light_fails_review(pa) &&
17238:                      !verdigris::gpu::washed_light_fails_review(pb),
17239:                  "material-light: moving samples stay under the channel cap");
17240:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::hud_label_alone_fails_light_review(false),
17241:                  "material-light: a HUD token without a pool cannot certify");
17242:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17243:   if (dir.empty()) {
17244:     scenario_check(false, "material-light: capture root rejected before any write");
17245:     sample.shutdown();
17246:     return scenario_failures;
17247:   }
17248:   scenario_check(sample.write_bmp(dir + "\\material-light-quad.bmp"),
17249:                  "material-light: damage-zone quad capture written");
17250:   sample.shutdown();
17251:   ClientState state;
17252:   scenario_begin(state);
17253:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17254:   state.breathe_phase = 0.62;
17255:   scenario_present(state);
17256:   bool named = false;
17257:   bool pool = false;
17258:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17259:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
17260:     if (item.label == "material-light:moving") named = true;
17261:     if (item.label == "material-light:pool") pool = true;
17262:   }
17263:   scenario_check(named, "material-light: live HUD names the moving light");
17264:   scenario_check(pool, "material-light: live scene paints a bronze light pool");
17265:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::hud_label_alone_fails_light_review(pool),
17266:                  "material-light: the pool, not a HUD token, certifies the light");
17267:   const std::string png = dir + "\\material-light-960x600.png";
17268:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17269:                  "material-light: live HUD capture written");
17270:   return scenario_failures;
17271: }

17273: int scenario_gpu_capture() {
17274:   const std::string log = verdigris::gpu::snapshot_text({});
17275:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::semantic_log_counts_as_capture("Hud 0 0 0 0 floor\n") &&
17276:                      !verdigris::gpu::semantic_log_counts_as_capture(log),
17277:                  "gpu-capture: a semantic draw log is not pixel evidence");
17278:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::swapped_png_rejected(69, 208, 208, 69),
17279:                  "gpu-capture: an R/B swapped still cannot certify pixels");
17280:   scenario_check(!verdigris::gpu::swapped_png_rejected(208, 69, 208, 69),
17281:                  "gpu-capture: matching channels are not treated as swapped");
17282:   verdigris::gpu::Bindings bind{};
17283:   verdigris::gpu::load_bindings(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software,
17284:                                 verdigris::gpu::kBindingLayoutVersion, &bind);
17285:   verdigris::gpu::Sample sample;
17286:   sample.init(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software);
17287:   verdigris::gpu::draw_lit_quad_moving(sample, bind,
17288:                                        verdigris::gpu::light_from_tick(4), true);
17289:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17290:   if (dir.empty()) {
17291:     scenario_check(false, "gpu-capture: capture root rejected before any write");
17292:     sample.shutdown();
17293:     return scenario_failures;
17294:   }
17295:   const std::string bmp = dir + "\\gpu-capture-readback.bmp";
17296:   const std::string prov = dir + "\\gpu-capture-readback.txt";
17297:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::capture_sample(sample, bmp, prov),
17298:                  "gpu-capture: readback wrote image and provenance");
17299:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::file_is_bmp(bmp),
17300:                  "gpu-capture: the image file is a usable BMP");
17301:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::provenance_complete(prov),
17302:                  "gpu-capture: provenance names backend, content, and platform");
17303:   sample.shutdown();
17304:   return scenario_failures;
17305: }
17306: 
17307: int scenario_gpu_recover() {
17308:   verdigris::gpu::RecoverablePresenter gpu;
17309:   scenario_check(gpu.recreate(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software, 64, 64) &&
17310:                      gpu.live_buffers == 1,
17311:                  "gpu-recover: first present allocates one pixel buffer");
17312:   scenario_check(gpu.recreate(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software, 128, 96) &&
17313:                      gpu.live_buffers == 1 && gpu.sample.width == 128,
17314:                  "gpu-recover: resize keeps a single live buffer");
17315:   scenario_check(gpu.recreate(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software, 64, 64) &&
17316:                      gpu.live_buffers == 1,
17317:                  "gpu-recover: restore to 64 does not leak the larger buffer");
17318:   for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i)
17319:     scenario_check(gpu.minimize_restore() && gpu.live_buffers == 1,
17320:                    "gpu-recover: minimize/restore cannot leak textures");
17321:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17322:   if (dir.empty()) {
17323:     scenario_check(false, "gpu-recover: capture root rejected before any write");
17324:     return scenario_failures;
17325:   }
17326:   gpu.sample.draw_textured_quad();
17327:   scenario_check(gpu.sample.write_bmp(dir + "\\gpu-recover-quad.bmp"),
17328:                  "gpu-recover: restored buffer capture written");
17329:   const std::string report = dir + "\\gpu-recover-report.txt";
17330:   FILE* out = nullptr;
17331:   fopen_s(&out, report.c_str(), "w");
17332:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "gpu-recover: report opened");
17333:   if (out) {
17334:     std::fprintf(out, "live_buffers=%d\ngeneration=%d\nerror_visible=%d\n",
17335:                  gpu.live_buffers, gpu.generation, gpu.error_visible ? 1 : 0);
17336:     std::fclose(out);
17337:   }
17338:   scenario_check(!gpu.recreate(verdigris::gpu::Backend::Software, 0, 0) &&
17339:                      gpu.live_buffers == 0 && gpu.error_visible &&
17340:                      std::strcmp(gpu.error, verdigris::gpu::kRecreateError) == 0,
17341:                  "gpu-recover: a failed recreate shows gpu-error:recreate");
17342:   scenario_check(!gpu.sample.alive,
17343:                  "gpu-recover: failure releases the previous buffer instead of crashing");
17344:   FILE* fail = nullptr;
17345:   fopen_s(&fail, report.c_str(), "a");
17346:   if (fail) {
17347:     std::fprintf(fail, "fail_error=%s\nfail_live=%d\n", gpu.error, gpu.live_buffers);
17348:     std::fclose(fail);
17349:   }
17350:   return scenario_failures;
17351: }
17352: 
17353: int scenario_sound_adapter() {
17354:   verdigris::audio::ToneAdapter adapter;
17355:   scenario_check(!adapter.init(static_cast<verdigris::audio::ToneBackend>(9)),
17356:                  "sound-adapter: an unknown backend cannot pretend to be portable");
17357:   scenario_check(adapter.init(verdigris::audio::ToneBackend::Software) &&
17358:                      adapter.play_generated_tone(440, 80, 800) && adapter.audible(),
17359:                  "sound-adapter: a generated test tone plays on the software adapter");
17360:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(verdigris::audio::ToneAdapter::kBackendName, "software") == 0,
17361:                  "sound-adapter: the decision is software, not a Windows-only device");
17362:   adapter.shutdown();
17363:   scenario_check(!adapter.alive && adapter.pcm.empty() &&
17364:                      !adapter.play_generated_tone(440, 80, 800),
17365:                  "sound-adapter: shutdown releases the buffer");
17366:   verdigris::audio::CueSpec silent;
17367:   silent.cue_id = "ghost";
17368:   silent.params = {verdigris::audio::Waveform::Sine, 440, 440, 0, 800};
17369:   scenario_check(!verdigris::audio::cue_has_audible_output(silent),
17370:                  "sound-adapter: a scheduled cue without duration is not audible output");
17371:   return scenario_failures;
17372: }

17374: int scenario_audio_prefs() {
17375:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17376:   if (dir.empty()) {
17377:     scenario_check(false, "audio-prefs: capture root rejected before any write");
17378:     return scenario_failures;
17379:   }
17380:   const std::string path = dir + "\\audio-prefs-test.txt";
17381:   verdigris::audio::AudioPrefs prefs;
17382:   prefs.muted = false;
17383:   prefs.sfx_permille = 400;
17384:   prefs.music_permille = 700;
17385:   scenario_check(verdigris::audio::save_audio_prefs(path, prefs),
17386:                  "audio-prefs: category volumes persist");
17387:   prefs = verdigris::audio::apply_mute_only(prefs, true);
17388:   verdigris::audio::save_audio_prefs(path, prefs);
17389:   const verdigris::audio::AudioPrefs loaded = verdigris::audio::load_audio_prefs(path);
17390:   scenario_check(loaded.muted && loaded.sfx_permille == 400 &&
17391:                      loaded.music_permille == 700,
17392:                  "audio-prefs: mute cannot reset unrelated category volumes");
17393:   scenario_check(verdigris::audio::mute_chip_alone_fails_prefs_review(false),
17394:                  "audio-prefs: a mute chip without the mixer cannot certify");
17395:   ClientState state;
17396:   scenario_begin(state);
17397:   ensure_audio(state);
17398:   state.audio_prefs = loaded;
17399:   verdigris::audio::apply_audio_prefs(*state.audio_mixer, loaded);
17400:   state.audio_mixer->set_bus_volume(verdigris::audio::Bus::Sfx, 0);
17401:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent hit{};
17402:   hit.type = verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::DamageApplied;
17403:   hit.value = 4;
17404:   std::vector<std::string> batch;
17405:   voice_presentation_event(state, hit, 3, batch);
17406:   const auto voiced = state.audio_mixer->drain_scheduled();
17407:   scenario_check(voiced.empty(),
17408:                  "audio-prefs: a zero-volume SFX category remains silent");
17409:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17410:   drain_audio(state);
17411:   scenario_present(state);
17412:   bool mixer = false;
17413:   bool sfx = false;
17414:   bool music = false;
17415:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17416:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
17417:     if (item.label == "audio:mixer") mixer = true;
17418:     if (item.label == "audio:sfx:400") sfx = true;
17419:     if (item.label == "audio:music:700") music = true;
17420:   }
17421:   scenario_check(mixer && sfx && music,
17422:                  "audio-prefs: live mixer paints persisted SFX and Music volumes");
17423:   scenario_check(!verdigris::audio::mute_chip_alone_fails_prefs_review(mixer),
17424:                  "audio-prefs: the mixer panel, not a mute chip, certifies prefs");
17425:   const std::string png = dir + "\\audio-prefs-960x600.png";
17426:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17427:                  "audio-prefs: muted HUD capture written");
17428:   return scenario_failures;
17429: }

17431: int scenario_ambience_layer() {
17432:   ClientState state;
17433:   scenario_begin(state);
17434:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17435:   refresh_ambience(state);
17436:   scenario_present(state);
17437:   bool hud = false;
17438:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17439:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("ambience:", 0) == 0)
17440:       hud = true;
17441:   scenario_check(hud, "ambience-layer: live HUD names the current zone loop");
17442:   state.audio_voiced.clear();
17443:   refresh_ambience(state);
17444:   drain_audio(state);
17445:   int first = 0;
17446:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced)
17447:     if (cue.rfind("ambience:", 0) == 0) ++first;
17448:   scenario_check(first <= 1, "ambience-layer: enter voices one region loop");
17449:   state.world.route_id = "route:salt:1:0";
17450:   refresh_ambience(state);
17451:   refresh_ambience(state);
17452:   state.audio_voiced.clear();
17453:   drain_audio(state);
17454:   int second = 0;
17455:   bool salt = false;
17456:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced) {
17457:     if (cue.rfind("ambience:", 0) == 0) ++second;
17458:     if (cue == "ambience:route:salt:1:0") salt = true;
17459:   }
17460:   scenario_check(second == 1 && salt,
17461:                  "ambience-layer: rapid zone reentry cannot stack loops");
17462:   return scenario_failures;
17463: }

17465: int scenario_equipment() {
17466:   verdigris::client::ui::EquipView view;
17467:   verdigris::client::ui::request_equip(view, "ghost-blade");
17468:   scenario_check(view.pending && view.acknowledged_id.empty(),
17469:                  "equipment: a request is pending until the sim acknowledges");
17470:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::ui::paints_optimistic_success(view),
17471:                  "equipment: pending cannot paint as an equipped success");
17472:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::ui::leaky_pending_as_equipped(view, "ghost-blade"),
17473:                  "equipment: treating the pending id as equipped is the anti-pattern");
17474:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::ui::paint_focus_as_equipped(view, true),
17475:                  "equipment: production compare cannot use that leak");
17476:   verdigris::client::ui::reject_equip(view);
17477:   scenario_check(!view.pending && view.acknowledged_id.empty(),
17478:                  "equipment: a rejected equip leaves the prior seat unchanged");
17479:   verdigris::client::ui::ack_equip(view, "bronze-edge", 4);
17480:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::ui::compare_delta(view, 7) == 3,
17481:                  "equipment: comparison uses the acknowledged attack");
17482:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::ui::compare_baseline(view, 99) == 4,
17483:                  "equipment: compare baseline is the ack, not a world guess");
17484:   paper_doll::State doll;
17485:   paper_doll::Item helm{1, paper_doll::Kind::Helmet, false};
17486:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::ui::try_slot(doll, helm,
17487:                                                  paper_doll::Slot::MainHand) ==
17488:                      paper_doll::Status::WrongSlot,
17489:                  "equipment: a helmet cannot occupy the main-hand seat");
17490:   ClientState state;
17491:   scenario_begin(state);
17492:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17493:   verdigris::client::ui::request_equip(state.equip_view, "ghost-blade");
17494:   scenario_present(state);
17495:   bool pending = false;
17496:   bool ok = false;
17497:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17498:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
17499:     if (item.label.rfind("equip:pending:", 0) == 0) pending = true;
17500:     if (item.label == "equip:ok") ok = true;
17501:   }
17502:   scenario_check(pending && !ok,
17503:                  "equipment: live HUD cannot pretend a rejected request succeeded");
17504: 
17505:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
17506:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
17507:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
17508:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
17509:   if (!state.simulation->ground_items().empty())
17510:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::pick_up(
17511:                              state.simulation->ground_items().front().id));
17512:   scenario_check(!state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty(),
17513:                  "equipment: pickup yields a compare candidate");
17514:   scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::unequip());
17515:   const std::string carried_id =
17516:       state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty()
17517:           ? std::string{}
17518:           : state.simulation->scion().carried_items.front().id;
17519:   verdigris::client::ui::request_equip(state.equip_view, carried_id);
17520:   state.gear_overlay = true;
17521:   scenario_present(state);
17522:   bool compare_pending = false;
17523:   bool compare_equipped = false;
17524:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17525:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
17526:     if (item.label == "compare:pending") compare_pending = true;
17527:     if (item.label == "compare:equipped") compare_equipped = true;
17528:   }
17529:   scenario_check(compare_pending && !compare_equipped,
17530:                  "equipment: pending compare cannot gold-frame as equipped");
17531: 
17532:   if (!carried_id.empty())
17533:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::equip(carried_id));
17534:   state.gear_overlay = true;
17535:   scenario_present(state);
17536:   bool ack_ok = false;
17537:   bool gold_equipped = false;
17538:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17539:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
17540:     if (item.label == "equip:ok") ack_ok = true;
17541:     if (item.label == "compare:equipped") gold_equipped = true;
17542:   }
17543:   scenario_check(ack_ok, "equipment: ack paints equip:ok");
17544:   scenario_check(gold_equipped,
17545:                  "equipment: compare plate follows the acknowledged seat");
17546:   bool tree_protocol = false;
17547:   bool tree_owner_absent = false;
17548:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17549:     if (item.op == render::Op::PaneStat &&
17550:         item.label == "TREE no authoritative data")
17551:       tree_protocol = true;
17552:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "tree:owner-absent")
17553:       tree_owner_absent = true;
17554:   }
17555:   scenario_check(tree_protocol,
17556:                  "equipment: PaneStat still states TREE absence for TASK-0156");
17557:   scenario_check(tree_owner_absent,
17558:                  "equipment: owner paint is Skill tree absence, not TREE jargon");
17559:   bool pack_glyph = false;
17560:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
17561:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
17562:         (item.label == "pack-glyph:vector" ||
17563:          item.label == "pack-glyph:billboard"))
17564:       pack_glyph = true;
17565:   }
17566:   scenario_check(pack_glyph,
17567:                  "equipment: pack cells paint a weapon glyph, not a grey crate");
17568:   scenario_check(!vector_art::grey_pack_icon_fails_review(
17569:                      vector_art::kPackGlyphHasBlade,
17570:                      vector_art::kPackGlyphHasGuard),
17571:                  "equipment: a grey square cannot certify a carried weapon");
17572: 
17573:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17574:   if (dir.empty()) {
17575:     scenario_check(false, "equipment: capture root rejected before any write");
17576:     return scenario_failures;
17577:   }
17578:   const std::string png = dir + "\\equipment-960x600.png";
17579:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17580:                  "equipment: compare-plate capture written");
17581:   return scenario_failures;
17582: }

17584: int scenario_memory_soak() {
17585:   ClientState state;
17586:   scenario_begin(state);
17587:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17588:   verdigris::perf::SoakEnvelope env;
17589:   for (int i = 0; i < verdigris::perf::kSoakMinCycles; ++i) {
17590:     scenario_present_size(state, 1920, 1080);
17591:     scenario_present_size(state, 640, 400);
17592:     scenario_present_size(state, 960, 600);
17593:     for (int n = 0; n < 24; ++n) {
17594:       EffectFx fx;
17595:       fx.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Impact;
17596:       fx.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x + n);
17597:       fx.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
17598:       fx.ttl = 4;
17599:       add_effect(state, fx);
17600:     }
17601:     scenario_present(state);
17602:     const PresentationResources res = presentation_resources(state);
17603:     verdigris::perf::note_cycle(env, res.floor_bitmaps, res.effects, res.gdi_pens);
17604:   }
17605:   std::printf("    memory-soak: cycles %d | floor bitmaps %d | fx %d | pens %d\n",
17606:               env.cycles, env.max_floor_bitmaps, env.max_effects, env.max_pens);
17607:   scenario_check(verdigris::perf::soak_is_long_enough(env),
17608:                  "memory-soak: a short scene cannot establish the envelope");
17609:   scenario_check(verdigris::perf::envelope_bounded(
17610:                      env, 1, static_cast<int>(kMaxPresentationEffects),
17611:                      static_cast<int>(kMaxCachedPens)),
17612:                  "memory-soak: CPU/session resources stay inside the cap");
17613:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17614:   if (dir.empty()) {
17615:     scenario_check(false, "memory-soak: capture root rejected before any write");
17616:     return scenario_failures;
17617:   }
17618:   const std::string report = dir + "\\memory-soak-report.txt";
17619:   FILE* out = nullptr;
17620:   fopen_s(&out, report.c_str(), "w");
17621:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "memory-soak: report opened");
17622:   if (out) {
17623:     std::fprintf(out, "cycles=%d\nmax_floor=%d\nmax_fx=%d\nmax_pens=%d\n",
17624:                  env.cycles, env.max_floor_bitmaps, env.max_effects, env.max_pens);
17625:     std::fclose(out);
17626:   }
17627:   return scenario_failures;
17628: }

17630: int scenario_dense_mix() {
17631:   verdigris::audio::CueSpec preview;
17632:   preview.cue_id = "preview";
17633:   preview.params = {verdigris::audio::Waveform::Sine, 440, 440, 80, 800};
17634:   preview.effective_gain_permille = 800;
17635:   const auto isolated =
17636:       verdigris::audio::score_mix({preview}, true);
17637:   scenario_check(verdigris::audio::isolated_preview_cannot_pass(isolated) &&
17638:                      !verdigris::audio::mix_is_encounter(isolated),
17639:                  "dense-mix: an isolated tone preview cannot prove the combat mix");
17640: 
17641:   ClientState state;
17642:   scenario_begin(state);
17643:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17644:   refresh_ambience(state);
17645:   drain_audio(state);
17646:   const auto* scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
17647:   scenario_check(scion != nullptr, "dense-mix: session has a scion");
17648:   if (scion) {
17649:     const int melee = verdigris::world_scale::kMeleeRange;
17650:     state.simulation->spawn_monster({scion->position.x + melee, scion->position.y}, 1,
17651:                                     false);
17652:     state.simulation->spawn_monster(
17653:         {scion->position.x + melee, scion->position.y + melee}, 1, false);
17654:     state.simulation->spawn_monster({scion->position.x - melee, scion->position.y}, 2,
17655:                                     true);
17656:   }
17657:   scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Wait));
17658:   scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::WarCry));
17659:   for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i)
17660:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
17661:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent lost{};
17662:   lost.type = verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::ScionLost;
17663:   std::vector<std::string> batch;
17664:   voice_presentation_event(state, lost, state.world.tick, batch);
17665:   drain_audio(state);
17666:   const auto score =
17667:       verdigris::audio::score_mix(state.audio_tape->cues(), false);
17668:   std::printf("    dense-mix: cues %d unique %d peak %d floor %d ids %s\n",
17669:               score.cues, score.unique_ids, score.peak_gain, score.floor_gain,
17670:               score.attribution.c_str());
17671:   scenario_check(verdigris::audio::mix_is_encounter(score) && score.has_warning,
17672:                  "dense-mix: mixed pack records hit, range, and a danger cue");
17673:   scenario_check(!score.attribution.empty() &&
17674:                      score.attribution.find("preview") == std::string::npos,
17675:                  "dense-mix: cue attribution names encounter voices, not a preview");
17676:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17677:   if (dir.empty()) {
17678:     scenario_check(false, "dense-mix: capture root rejected before any write");
17679:     return scenario_failures;
17680:   }
17681:   scenario_check(verdigris::audio::write_mix_score(dir + "\\dense-mix-score.txt", score),
17682:                  "dense-mix: review record written");
17683:   const std::string png = dir + "\\dense-mix-960x600.png";
17684:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17685:                  "dense-mix: mixed-pack capture written");
17686:   return scenario_failures;
17687: }

17689: int scenario_pane_stack() {
17690:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::ui::helper_depth_alone_cannot_prove(2, false, false),
17691:                  "pane-stack: helper depth without native paint/Escape is the anti-pattern");
17692:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::ui::helper_depth_alone_cannot_prove(2, true, true),
17693:                  "pane-stack: native paint plus Escape can prove the journey");
17694: 
17695:   ClientState state;
17696:   scenario_begin(state);
17697:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17698:   toggle_gear_overlay(state);
17699:   state.character_pane = true;
17700:   scenario_present(state);
17701:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::ui::pane_stack_depth(
17702:                      state.tree_pane, state.character_pane, state.gear_overlay) == 2,
17703:                  "pane-stack: gear and character are both owned");
17704:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::PaneStat) &&
17705:                      render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::PaneWeapon),
17706:                  "pane-stack: native shell paints the gear pane");
17707:   bool stack2 = false;
17708:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17709:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "pane-stack:2") stack2 = true;
17710:   scenario_check(stack2, "pane-stack: live HUD names depth 2, not only the helper");
17711:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17712:   if (dir.empty()) {
17713:     scenario_check(false, "pane-stack: capture root rejected before any write");
17714:     return scenario_failures;
17715:   }
17716:   const std::string png = dir + "\\pane-stack-960x600.png";
17717:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17718:                  "pane-stack: stacked-pane capture written");
17719: 
17720:   handle_escape_key(state);
17721:   scenario_check(state.gear_overlay && !state.character_pane && !state.quit_requested,
17722:                  "pane-stack: first Escape dismisses the top pane only");
17723:   scenario_present(state);
17724:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::PaneStat) &&
17725:                      render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::PaneWeapon),
17726:                  "pane-stack: gear remains after the character sheet closes");
17727:   bool stack1 = false;
17728:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17729:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "pane-stack:1") stack1 = true;
17730:   scenario_check(stack1, "pane-stack: HUD drops to depth 1 after Escape");
17731:   handle_escape_key(state);
17732:   scenario_check(!state.gear_overlay && !state.quit_requested,
17733:                  "pane-stack: second Escape closes gear without quitting");
17734:   handle_escape_key(state);
17735:   scenario_check(state.quit_requested,
17736:                  "pane-stack: bare Escape requests application exit");
17737: 
17738:   ClientState tree;
17739:   scenario_begin(tree);
17740:   scenario_follow_camera(tree);
17741:   tree.tree_pane = true;
17742:   scenario_present(tree);
17743:   bool tree_pane = false;
17744:   bool owner_title = false;
17745:   bool owner_absent = false;
17746:   for (const auto& item : tree.render_list) {
17747:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
17748:     if (item.label == "tree-pane") tree_pane = true;
17749:     if (item.label == "tree:owner-title") owner_title = true;
17750:     if (item.label == "tree:owner-absent") owner_absent = true;
17751:   }
17752:   scenario_check(tree_pane && owner_title && owner_absent,
17753:                  "pane-stack: skill tree paints owner title and absence");
17754:   const std::string tree_png = dir + "\\tree-pane-960x600.png";
17755:   scenario_check(reference_present(tree, 960, 600, tree_png),
17756:                  "pane-stack: skill-tree capture written");
17757:   return scenario_failures;
17758: }

17760: int scenario_pane_focus() {
17761:   ClientState state;
17762:   scenario_begin(state);
17763:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17764:   RECT bounds{0, 0, 960, 600};
17765:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
17766:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17767:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
17768:     return false;
17769:   };
17770: 
17771:   const verdigris::Vec2 origin = state.world.player.position;
17772:   state.d = true;
17773:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
17774:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17775:   scenario_present(state);
17776:   scenario_check(state.world.player.position.x > origin.x,
17777:                  "pane-focus: WASD moves when no pane holds focus");
17778:   scenario_check(has_hud("focus:none"),
17779:                  "pane-focus: empty stack publishes focus:none");
17780:   state.d = false;
17781: 
17782:   toggle_gear_overlay(state);
17783:   scenario_present(state);
17784:   scenario_check(has_hud("focus:gear"),
17785:                  "pane-focus: gear overlay is the focused surface");
17786:   const verdigris::Vec2 parked = state.world.player.position;
17787:   const int combat0 = state.combat_requests;
17788:   state.d = true;
17789:   state.w = true;
17790:   state.pack_drag_live = true;
17791:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
17792:   dispatch_skill(state, kStrike);
17793:   dispatch_dash(state);
17794:   scenario_check(state.world.player.position.x == parked.x &&
17795:                      state.world.player.position.y == parked.y,
17796:                  "pane-focus: click/drag over gear cannot move the Scion");
17797:   scenario_check(state.combat_requests == combat0,
17798:                  "pane-focus: combat keys are consumed while gear is focused");
17799:   scenario_check(state.attack_held_blocked,
17800:                  "pane-focus: held attack is remembered as blocked");
17801:   state.pack_drag_live = false;
17802:   state.d = false;
17803:   state.w = false;
17804: 
17805:   state.text_entry = true;
17806:   scenario_present(state);
17807:   scenario_check(has_hud("focus:text"),
17808:                  "pane-focus: text-entry sits above gear and swallows input");
17809:   handle_escape_key(state);
17810:   scenario_check(!state.text_entry && state.gear_overlay,
17811:                  "pane-focus: Escape closes text without dropping gear");
17812: 
17813:   toggle_gear_overlay(state);
17814:   scenario_check(!state.gear_overlay, "pane-focus: gear closed after text");
17815:   const int combat1 = state.combat_requests;
17816:   dispatch_skill(state, kStrike);
17817:   scenario_check(state.combat_requests == combat1,
17818:                  "pane-focus: closing a pane does not release a buffered attack");
17819:   release_held_gameplay_attack(state);
17820:   dispatch_skill(state, kStrike);
17821:   scenario_check(state.combat_requests == combat1 + 1,
17822:                  "pane-focus: a fresh attack after key-up reaches gameplay");
17823: 
17824:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17825:   if (dir.empty()) {
17826:     scenario_check(false, "pane-focus: capture root rejected before any write");
17827:     return scenario_failures;
17828:   }
17829:   toggle_gear_overlay(state);
17830:   scenario_present(state);
17831:   const std::string png = dir + "\\pane-focus-960x600.png";
17832:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17833:                  "pane-focus: focused gear capture written");
17834:   return scenario_failures;
17835: }

17837: int scenario_remap_binds() {
17838:   namespace binds = verdigris::client::input;
17839:   ClientState state;
17840:   scenario_begin(state);
17841:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17842:   RECT bounds{0, 0, 960, 600};
17843:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
17844:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17845:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
17846:     return false;
17847:   };
17848: 
17849:   const std::string owner =
17850:       "C:\\Users\\Owner\\Documents\\verdigris\\bindings.v1";
17851:   scenario_check(binds::path_is_owner_profile(owner),
17852:                  "remap-binds: Documents is classified as an owner profile");
17853:   scenario_check(binds::save_bindings(owner, binds::default_bindings()) ==
17854:                      binds::BindStatus::OwnerProfile,
17855:                  "remap-binds: owner Documents is never the test destination");
17856: 
17857:   binds::Bindings clash = binds::default_bindings();
17858:   scenario_check(binds::remap(clash, binds::Action::Dash, binds::Device::Keyboard,
17859:                               'W') == binds::BindStatus::Conflict,
17860:                  "remap-binds: duplicate WASD/dash codes fail closed");
17861:   state.bind_status = binds::BindStatus::Conflict;
17862:   scenario_present(state);
17863:   scenario_check(has_hud("bind:conflict"),
17864:                  "remap-binds: conflict is visible on the live HUD");
17865:   scenario_check(state.bindings.dash == 0x20,
17866:                  "remap-binds: a refused remap cannot mutate the live set");
17867: 
17868:   scenario_check(binds::remap(clash, binds::Action::Dash,
17869:                               static_cast<binds::Device>(9), 'G') ==
17870:                      binds::BindStatus::InvalidDevice,
17871:                  "remap-binds: unknown device codes fail visibly");
17872:   state.bind_status = binds::BindStatus::InvalidDevice;
17873:   scenario_present(state);
17874:   scenario_check(has_hud("bind:invalid-device"),
17875:                  "remap-binds: invalid device is named on the HUD");
17876: 
17877:   const std::string path = isolated_bindings_path();
17878:   scenario_check(!path.empty() && !binds::path_is_owner_profile(path),
17879:                  "remap-binds: isolated test profile is not the owner profile");
17880:   state.bind_status = binds::remap(state.bindings, binds::Action::Dash,
17881:                                    binds::Device::Keyboard, 'G');
17882:   scenario_check(state.bind_status == binds::BindStatus::Ok,
17883:                  "remap-binds: dash can move to G");
17884:   scenario_check(binds::save_bindings(path, state.bindings) == binds::BindStatus::Ok,
17885:                  "remap-binds: remapped set writes to the isolated profile");
17886: 
17887:   ClientState restarted;
17888:   scenario_begin(restarted);
17889:   restarted.bind_status = binds::load_bindings(path, restarted.bindings);
17890:   scenario_check(restarted.bind_status == binds::BindStatus::Ok &&
17891:                      restarted.bindings.dash == 'G',
17892:                  "remap-binds: restart reloads the remapped dash");
17893:   const int before_space = restarted.combat_requests;
17894:   apply_bound_key_down(restarted, VK_SPACE);
17895:   scenario_check(restarted.combat_requests == before_space,
17896:                  "remap-binds: default Space is inaccessible after remap");
17897:   apply_bound_key_down(restarted, 'G');
17898:   scenario_check(restarted.combat_requests == before_space + 1,
17899:                  "remap-binds: remapped G still dashes after restart");
17900: 
17901:   restarted.bindings = binds::default_bindings();
17902:   restarted.bind_status = binds::BindStatus::Ok;
17903:   const int before_restore = restarted.combat_requests;
17904:   apply_bound_key_down(restarted, VK_SPACE);
17905:   scenario_check(restarted.combat_requests == before_restore + 1,
17906:                  "remap-binds: restore defaults returns Space dash");
17907:   scenario_present(restarted);
17908:   auto has_ok = false;
17909:   for (const auto& item : restarted.render_list)
17910:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == std::string("bind:ok"))
17911:       has_ok = true;
17912:   scenario_check(has_ok, "remap-binds: restored set paints bind:ok");
17913: 
17914:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17915:   if (dir.empty()) {
17916:     scenario_check(false, "remap-binds: capture root rejected before any write");
17917:     return scenario_failures;
17918:   }
17919:   const std::string png = dir + "\\remap-binds-960x600.png";
17920:   scenario_check(reference_present(restarted, 960, 600, png),
17921:                  "remap-binds: restored-binding capture written");
17922:   DeleteFileA(path.c_str());
17923:   return scenario_failures;
17924: }

17926: int scenario_attack_beat() {
17927:   ClientState state;
17928:   scenario_begin(state);
17929:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
17930:   scenario_present(state);
17931:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
17932:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
17933:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
17934:     return false;
17935:   };
17936:   scenario_check(has_hud("attack-beat:none"),
17937:                  "attack-beat: idle session publishes none");
17938: 
17939:   EffectFx fake;
17940:   fake.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Swing;
17941:   fake.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x);
17942:   fake.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
17943:   fake.ttl = 6;
17944:   add_effect(state, fake);
17945:   scenario_present(state);
17946:   scenario_check(has_hud("attack-beat:none") && state.attack_beat_trace.empty(),
17947:                  "attack-beat: a swing sprite without events cannot mint a beat");
17948: 
17949:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
17950:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
17951:   bool saw_anticipate = false;
17952:   bool saw_impact = false;
17953:   bool saw_aftermath = false;
17954:   for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
17955:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
17956:     for (const auto& label : state.attack_beat_trace) {
17957:       if (label == std::string("attack-beat:anticipate")) saw_anticipate = true;
17958:       if (label == std::string("attack-beat:impact")) saw_impact = true;
17959:       if (label == std::string("attack-beat:aftermath")) saw_aftermath = true;
17960:     }
17961:   }
17962:   scenario_present(state);
17963:   drain_audio(state);
17964:   bool voiced_anticipate = false;
17965:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced)
17966:     if (cue == "attack-anticipate") voiced_anticipate = true;
17967:   scenario_check(saw_anticipate,
17968:                  "attack-beat: AttackStarted advances anticipation");
17969:   scenario_check(voiced_anticipate,
17970:                  "attack-beat: anticipation submits an attack-anticipate cue");
17971:   scenario_check(saw_impact, "attack-beat: DamageApplied advances impact");
17972:   scenario_check(saw_aftermath || has_hud("attack-beat:impact") ||
17973:                      has_hud("attack-beat:aftermath"),
17974:                  "attack-beat: impact or aftermath remains on the live HUD");
17975: 
17976:   ClientState cancel_state;
17977:   scenario_begin(cancel_state);
17978:   scenario_follow_camera(cancel_state);
17979:   cancel_state.attack_beat = verdigris::client::combat::AttackBeat::Anticipate;
17980:   verdigris::Event dash{};
17981:   dash.type = verdigris::EventType::ActorMoved;
17982:   dash.text = "dash";
17983:   note_attack_beat(cancel_state, dash);
17984:   scenario_present(cancel_state);
17985:   bool cancel_hud = false;
17986:   for (const auto& item : cancel_state.render_list)
17987:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == std::string("attack-beat:cancel"))
17988:       cancel_hud = true;
17989:   scenario_check(cancel_hud,
17990:                  "attack-beat: dash during anticipation is cancel, not impact");
17991: 
17992:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
17993:   if (dir.empty()) {
17994:     scenario_check(false, "attack-beat: capture root rejected before any write");
17995:     return scenario_failures;
17996:   }
17997:   const std::string png = dir + "\\attack-beat-960x600.png";
17998:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
17999:                  "attack-beat: live beat capture written");
18000:   return scenario_failures;
18001: }
