18003: int scenario_dressing_pass() {
18004:   ClientState state;
18005:   scenario_begin(state);
18006:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18007:   auto fill_samples = [&](verdigris::client::world::LayoutSample* samples,
18008:                           std::size_t cap) {
18009:     const std::size_t count = std::min(state.scenery.size(), cap);
18010:     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
18011:       const SceneryItem& item = state.scenery[i];
18012:       samples[i].kind = static_cast<int>(item.kind);
18013:       samples[i].x = item.position.x;
18014:       samples[i].y = item.position.y;
18015:       samples[i].radius = static_cast<int>(item.radius);
18016:       samples[i].solid = item.solid;
18017:       samples[i].dressing = item.dressing;
18018:     }
18019:     return count;
18020:   };
18021:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
18022:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18023:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
18024:     return false;
18025:   };
18026: 
18027:   state.dressing_pass_version = 1;
18028:   generate_scenery(state);
18029:   verdigris::client::world::LayoutSample samples[128];
18030:   std::size_t count = fill_samples(samples, 128);
18031:   const std::string route = state.simulation->instance().route_id;
18032:   const std::uint64_t reward = scenery_seed(route);
18033:   const verdigris::Vec2 spawn = state.world.player.position;
18034:   const std::uint64_t topo_v1 = verdigris::client::world::topology_hash(
18035:       samples, count, spawn.x, spawn.y, reward);
18036:   const std::uint64_t dress_v1 = verdigris::client::world::dressing_hash(
18037:       samples, count, 1);
18038:   scenario_present(state);
18039:   scenario_check(has_hud("dressing-pass:v1"),
18040:                  "dressing-pass: version 1 is named on the HUD");
18041:   scenario_check(has_hud("dressing:tree"),
18042:                  "dressing-pass: dressing trees are labeled separately from solids");
18043:   scenario_check(state.topology_hash == topo_v1,
18044:                  "dressing-pass: live topology hash matches the frozen layout");
18045: 
18046:   int dressing_n = 0;
18047:   SceneryItem* first_dress = nullptr;
18048:   for (auto& item : state.scenery)
18049:     if (item.dressing) {
18050:       ++dressing_n;
18051:       if (!first_dress) first_dress = &item;
18052:     }
18053:   scenario_check(dressing_n == 2, "dressing-pass: v1 plants two dressing trees");
18054:   scenario_check(first_dress && !first_dress->solid,
18055:                  "dressing-pass: production dressing is never solid");
18056:   const verdigris::Vec2 from = spawn;
18057:   const verdigris::Vec2 into_dress{first_dress->position.x, first_dress->position.y};
18058:   scenario_check(!scenery_blocks_segment(state, from, into_dress),
18059:                  "dressing-pass: a tree visual does not block the approved layout");
18060: 
18061:   first_dress->solid = true;
18062:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::world::dressing_is_unreported_obstacle(
18063:                      {0, first_dress->position.x, first_dress->position.y,
18064:                       static_cast<int>(first_dress->radius), true, true}),
18065:                  "dressing-pass: a solid dressing tree is an unreported obstacle");
18066:   scenario_check(scenery_blocks_segment(state, from, into_dress),
18067:                  "dressing-pass: illegally solid dressing would collide");
18068:   first_dress->solid = false;
18069: 
18070:   state.dressing_pass_version = 2;
18071:   generate_scenery(state);
18072:   count = fill_samples(samples, 128);
18073:   const std::uint64_t topo_v2 = verdigris::client::world::topology_hash(
18074:       samples, count, state.world.player.position.x,
18075:       state.world.player.position.y, scenery_seed(route));
18076:   const std::uint64_t dress_v2 = verdigris::client::world::dressing_hash(
18077:       samples, count, 2);
18078:   scenario_present(state);
18079:   scenario_check(topo_v2 == topo_v1,
18080:                  "dressing-pass: art version cannot change collision topology");
18081:   scenario_check(state.world.player.position.x == spawn.x &&
18082:                      state.world.player.position.y == spawn.y,
18083:                  "dressing-pass: spawn is unchanged across dressing versions");
18084:   scenario_check(scenery_seed(route) == reward,
18085:                  "dressing-pass: reward/layout seed is unchanged");
18086:   scenario_check(dress_v2 != dress_v1,
18087:                  "dressing-pass: the decoration pass itself did change");
18088:   scenario_check(has_hud("dressing-pass:v2"),
18089:                  "dressing-pass: version 2 is named on the HUD");
18090: 
18091:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18092:   if (dir.empty()) {
18093:     scenario_check(false, "dressing-pass: capture root rejected before any write");
18094:     return scenario_failures;
18095:   }
18096:   const std::string png = dir + "\\dressing-pass-960x600.png";
18097:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18098:                  "dressing-pass: versioned dressing capture written");
18099:   return scenario_failures;
18100: }
18101: 
18102: int scenario_loot_filter() {
18103:   ClientState state;
18104:   scenario_begin(state);
18105:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18106:   const verdigris::Vec2 origin = state.world.player.position;
18107:   state.loot_labels = true;
18108:   state.world.loot_names["bronze-pike"] = "Bronze Pike";
18109:   state.world.loot_names["trophy-omen"] = "Bird Omen trophy";
18110:   state.world.loot_names["pouch-coin"] = "Coin pouch";
18111:   state.loot_positions["bronze-pike"] = {origin.x + 40, origin.y};
18112:   state.loot_positions["trophy-omen"] = {origin.x + 80, origin.y};
18113:   state.loot_positions["pouch-coin"] = {origin.x + 120, origin.y};
18114:   const std::size_t owned = state.loot_positions.size();
18115:   const std::size_t sim_items = state.simulation->ground_items().size();
18116:   const std::size_t sim_trophies = state.simulation->ground_trophies().size();
18117:   scenario_present(state);
18118:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
18119:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18120:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
18121:     return false;
18122:   };
18123:   auto drop_named = [&](const char* id) {
18124:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18125:       if (item.op == render::Op::Drop && item.label == id) return true;
18126:     return false;
18127:   };
18128:   scenario_check(has_hud("loot-filter:all"),
18129:                  "loot-filter: default filter publishes all categories");
18130:   scenario_check(has_hud("loot-fact:weapon") && has_hud("loot-fact:trophy") &&
18131:                      has_hud("loot-fact:misc"),
18132:                  "loot-filter: ground drops publish safe category facts");
18133:   scenario_check(drop_named("bronze-pike") && drop_named("trophy-omen") &&
18134:                      drop_named("pouch-coin"),
18135:                  "loot-filter: every pouch still draws as Drop");
18136:   scenario_check(has_hud("loot-label:bronze-pike") &&
18137:                      has_hud("loot-label:trophy-omen") &&
18138:                      has_hud("loot-label:pouch-coin"),
18139:                  "loot-filter: unfiltered nameplates cover each category");
18140: 
18141:   state.loot_filter.show_trophy = false;
18142:   scenario_present(state);
18143:   scenario_check(has_hud("loot-filter:hide:trophy"),
18144:                  "loot-filter: hiding trophies is named on the HUD");
18145:   scenario_check(has_hud("loot-fact:trophy"),
18146:                  "loot-filter: hidden trophies still publish their fact");
18147:   scenario_check(drop_named("trophy-omen"),
18148:                  "loot-filter: hiding a label cannot delete the Drop sprite");
18149:   scenario_check(!has_hud("loot-label:trophy-omen"),
18150:                  "loot-filter: trophy nameplates are suppressed");
18151:   scenario_check(has_hud("loot-label:bronze-pike") && has_hud("loot-label:pouch-coin"),
18152:                  "loot-filter: other categories stay readable");
18153:   scenario_check(state.loot_positions.size() == owned,
18154:                  "loot-filter: hiding cannot change ground ownership");
18155:   scenario_check(state.simulation->ground_items().size() == sim_items &&
18156:                      state.simulation->ground_trophies().size() == sim_trophies,
18157:                  "loot-filter: droprate/ownership tables stay untouched");
18158: 
18159:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18160:   if (dir.empty()) {
18161:     scenario_check(false, "loot-filter: capture root rejected before any write");
18162:     return scenario_failures;
18163:   }
18164:   const std::string png = dir + "\\loot-filter-960x600.png";
18165:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18166:                  "loot-filter: filtered nameplate capture written");
18167:   return scenario_failures;
18168: }

18170: int scenario_build_fixtures() {
18171:   ClientState state;
18172:   scenario_begin(state);
18173:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18174:   state.character_pane = true;
18175:   scenario_present(state);
18176:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
18177:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18178:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
18179:     return false;
18180:   };
18181:   auto has_prefix = [&](const char* prefix) {
18182:     const std::string p = prefix;
18183:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18184:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind(p, 0) == 0)
18185:         return true;
18186:     return false;
18187:   };
18188:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::builds::distinct_slice_loops(
18189:                      verdigris::client::builds::kSliceBuilds, 3),
18190:                  "build-fixtures: reach/pressure/magic are distinct loops");
18191:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::builds::tint_only_clones_fail_review(),
18192:                  "build-fixtures: three tinted copies of melee fail review");
18193:   scenario_check(has_hud("build-fixture:reach") &&
18194:                      has_hud("build-fixture:pressure") &&
18195:                      has_hud("build-fixture:magic"),
18196:                  "build-fixtures: character sheet names all three roles");
18197:   scenario_check(has_hud("build-loops:distinct") && has_hud("build-loops:tint-fail"),
18198:                  "build-fixtures: distinct-loop and tint-fail HUD flags");
18199:   scenario_check(has_prefix("build-tactics:reach:") &&
18200:                      has_prefix("build-weak:reach:") &&
18201:                      has_prefix("build-gear:reach:") &&
18202:                      has_prefix("build-answer:reach:"),
18203:                  "build-fixtures: reach lists tactics, weakness, gear, answer");
18204:   scenario_check(has_prefix("build-tactics:pressure:") &&
18205:                      has_prefix("build-weak:pressure:") &&
18206:                      has_prefix("build-gear:pressure:") &&
18207:                      has_prefix("build-answer:pressure:"),
18208:                  "build-fixtures: pressure lists tactics, weakness, gear, answer");
18209:   scenario_check(has_prefix("build-tactics:magic:") &&
18210:                      has_prefix("build-weak:magic:") &&
18211:                      has_prefix("build-gear:magic:") &&
18212:                      has_prefix("build-answer:magic:"),
18213:                  "build-fixtures: magic lists tactics, weakness, gear, answer");
18214:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18215:   if (dir.empty()) {
18216:     scenario_check(false, "build-fixtures: capture root rejected before any write");
18217:     return scenario_failures;
18218:   }
18219:   const std::string png = dir + "\\build-fixtures-960x600.png";
18220:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18221:                  "build-fixtures: character-sheet capture written");
18222:   return scenario_failures;
18223: }
18224: 
18225: int scenario_telegraph_spec() {
18226:   const auto catalog = verdigris::Simulation::presentation_catalog();
18227:   const auto local = verdigris::client::actions::spec_from_payload(
18228:       "thrust", catalog.telegraph_ticks, catalog);
18229:   const auto remote_ms = verdigris::client::actions::spec_from_payload(
18230:       "thrust", 1000, catalog);
18231:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::actions::same_warning(local, remote_ms),
18232:                  "telegraph-spec: local ticks and remote durationMs share the catalog window");
18233:   scenario_check(
18234:       verdigris::client::actions::millisecond_guess_diverges(1000, remote_ms),
18235:       "telegraph-spec: value/50 cannot invent a longer window");
18236:   scenario_check(local.duration_ticks == catalog.telegraph_ticks &&
18237:                      local.reach == catalog.thrust_range,
18238:                  "telegraph-spec: duration and thrust reach are catalog-typed");
18239:   const auto sweep = verdigris::client::actions::spec_from_payload("sweep", 3, catalog);
18240:   scenario_check(sweep.reach == catalog.melee_range,
18241:                  "telegraph-spec: sweep uses the melee footprint");
18242: 
18243:   verdigris::client::PresentationFx fx;
18244:   verdigris::client::WorldView world;
18245:   world.tick = 1;
18246:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent tel{
18247:       verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::Telegraph, "foe", "", "thrust",
18248:       1000};
18249:   verdigris::client::apply_presentation_event(fx, world, tel, 1);
18250:   scenario_check(fx.telegraphs.count("foe") == 1 &&
18251:                      fx.telegraphs["foe"].windup_ticks == catalog.telegraph_ticks &&
18252:                      fx.telegraphs["foe"].reach == catalog.thrust_range,
18253:                  "telegraph-spec: remote consumer stores the catalog window");
18254:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent started{
18255:       verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::AttackStarted, "foe", "", "thrust",
18256:       0};
18257:   verdigris::client::apply_presentation_event(fx, world, started, 2);
18258:   scenario_check(fx.telegraphs.count("foe") == 0,
18259:                  "telegraph-spec: AttackStarted cancels the warning");
18260: 
18261:   fx.telegraphs["stale"] = {};
18262:   fx.telegraphs["stale"].action = "thrust";
18263:   fx.telegraphs["stale"].start_tick = 0;
18264:   fx.telegraphs["stale"].windup_ticks = 1;
18265:   world.tick = 40;
18266:   camera2d::Camera cam{};
18267:   render::List remote_list;
18268:   verdigris::client::record_world_ops(remote_list, world, fx, cam, 960, 600);
18269:   bool remote_telegraph = false;
18270:   for (const auto& item : remote_list)
18271:     if (item.op == render::Op::Telegraph) remote_telegraph = true;
18272:   scenario_check(!remote_telegraph,
18273:                  "telegraph-spec: expired remote warning leaves no footprint");
18274: 
18275:   ClientState state;
18276:   scenario_begin(state);
18277:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18278:   const auto* scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18279:   scenario_check(scion != nullptr, "telegraph-spec: session has a scion");
18280:   if (scion) {
18281:     const int melee = verdigris::world_scale::kMeleeRange;
18282:     state.simulation->spawn_monster(
18283:         {scion->position.x - melee, scion->position.y}, 1, true);
18284:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Wait));
18285:   }
18286:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
18287:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18288:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
18289:     return false;
18290:   };
18291:   scenario_check(render::any(state.render_list, render::Op::Telegraph),
18292:                  "telegraph-spec: live elite publishes a warning");
18293:   const std::string typed_hud = verdigris::client::actions::spec_hud(local);
18294:   const std::string sweep_hud = verdigris::client::actions::spec_hud(sweep);
18295:   scenario_check(has_hud(typed_hud.c_str()) || has_hud(sweep_hud.c_str()),
18296:                  "telegraph-spec: HUD names the typed window and reach");
18297: 
18298:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18299:   if (dir.empty()) {
18300:     scenario_check(false, "telegraph-spec: capture root rejected before any write");
18301:     return scenario_failures;
18302:   }
18303:   const std::string png = dir + "\\telegraph-spec-960x600.png";
18304:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18305:                  "telegraph-spec: warning-window capture written");
18306: 
18307:   ActiveTelegraph ghost;
18308:   ghost.actor_id = "ghost";
18309:   ghost.action = "thrust";
18310:   ghost.position = state.world.player.position;
18311:   ghost.start_tick = 0;
18312:   ghost.windup_ticks = 1;
18313:   state.telegraphs["ghost"] = ghost;
18314:   state.world.tick = std::max<std::uint64_t>(state.world.tick, 40);
18315:   scenario_present(state);
18316:   scenario_check(has_hud("telegraph-ghost"),
18317:                  "telegraph-spec: an expired map entry cannot stay a silent cone");
18318:   const int removed = verdigris::client::actions::prune_expired_telegraphs(
18319:       state.telegraphs, state.world.tick);
18320:   scenario_check(removed >= 1, "telegraph-spec: prune drops expired warnings");
18321:   scenario_present(state);
18322:   scenario_check(!has_hud("telegraph-ghost"),
18323:                  "telegraph-spec: after prune there is no invisible damage telegraph");
18324:   return scenario_failures;
18325: }

18327: int scenario_ranged_warning() {
18328:   using verdigris::client::projectile::from_js_payload;
18329:   using verdigris::client::projectile::hud_chip;
18330:   using verdigris::client::projectile::is_projectile_warning;
18331:   using verdigris::client::projectile::kAuthoredVolleyTravelMs;
18332: 
18333:   ClientState state;
18334:   scenario_begin(state);
18335:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18336: 
18337:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent slam{
18338:       verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::Telegraph, "boss-1", "",
18339:       "boss:ground-slam", 800};
18340:   scenario_check(!is_projectile_warning(slam),
18341:                  "ranged-warning: slam envelope is not a projectile windup");
18342: 
18343:   const auto warning =
18344:       from_js_payload("flint-1", 1, 1, 5, 1, kAuthoredVolleyTravelMs, "monster");
18345:   verdigris::client::PresentationFx fx;
18346:   verdigris::client::apply_presentation_event(fx, state.world, warning,
18347:                                              state.world.tick);
18348:   scenario_check(fx.telegraphs.count("flint-1") == 1 &&
18349:                      fx.telegraphs["flint-1"].action == "projectile",
18350:                  "ranged-warning: projectile windup stores a Telegraph");
18351:   scenario_check(fx.telegraphs["flint-1"].windup_ticks ==
18352:                      kAuthoredVolleyTravelMs / verdigris::kSimulationTickMs,
18353:                  "ranged-warning: travelMs uses the authored 50ms tick");
18354: 
18355:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent hit{
18356:       verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::DamageApplied, "flint-1", "",
18357:       "incoming", 4};
18358:   verdigris::client::apply_presentation_event(fx, state.world, hit,
18359:                                              state.world.tick + 1);
18360:   state.telegraphs = fx.telegraphs;
18361:   state.effects = fx.effects;
18362:   scenario_present(state);
18363: 
18364:   bool saw_telegraph = false;
18365:   bool saw_damage = false;
18366:   bool saw_impact = false;
18367:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
18368:     if (item.op == render::Op::Telegraph) saw_telegraph = true;
18369:     if (item.op == render::Op::Damage) saw_damage = true;
18370:     if (item.op == render::Op::Impact || item.op == render::Op::TargetFlash)
18371:       saw_impact = true;
18372:   }
18373:   scenario_check(saw_telegraph,
18374:                  "ranged-warning: live list records Telegraph before the hit");
18375:   scenario_check(saw_damage && saw_impact,
18376:                  "ranged-warning: hit lands as attributed Damage/Impact");
18377:   scenario_check(fx.monster_strikes.count("flint-1") == 1,
18378:                  "ranged-warning: incoming damage is attributed to flint-1");
18379: 
18380:   verdigris::client::PresentationFx bare;
18381:   verdigris::client::apply_presentation_event(bare, state.world, hit, 9);
18382:   scenario_check(bare.telegraphs.find("flint-1") == bare.telegraphs.end(),
18383:                  "ranged-warning: a hit without a warning cannot mint Telegraph");
18384: 
18385:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18386:   if (dir.empty()) {
18387:     scenario_check(false, "ranged-warning: capture root rejected before any write");
18388:     return scenario_failures;
18389:   }
18390:   const std::string png = dir + "\\ranged-warning-960x600.png";
18391:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18392:                  "ranged-warning: capture written");
18393:   return scenario_failures;
18394: }

18396: int scenario_headless_contract() {
18397:   verdigris::client::qa::SemanticIntent intent{};
18398:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::qa::intent_for_attack_started(&intent) &&
18399:                      intent.voiced ==
18400:                          verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::AttackStarted,
18401:                  "headless-contract: AttackStarted maps to a typed intent");
18402: 
18403:   verdigris::Event started{};
18404:   started.type = verdigris::EventType::AttackStarted;
18405:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent voiced{};
18406:   const bool bridge_mapped = presentation_from_sim(started, voiced) &&
18407:                              voiced.type == intent.voiced;
18408:   scenario_check(bridge_mapped,
18409:                  "headless-contract: production bridge voices AttackStarted");
18410: 
18411:   auto broken_bridge = [](const verdigris::Event& event,
18412:                           verdigris::client::PresentationEvent& out) {
18413:     if (event.type == verdigris::EventType::AttackStarted) return false;
18414:     return presentation_from_sim(event, out);
18415:   };
18416:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent dropped{};
18417:   scenario_check(!broken_bridge(started, dropped),
18418:                  "headless-contract: removing the bridge fails the fixture");
18419: 
18420:   ClientState mock;
18421:   scenario_begin(mock);
18422:   scenario_follow_camera(mock);
18423:   verdigris::client::PresentationEvent fake{
18424:       verdigris::client::PresentationEventType::AttackStarted, "mock", "", "melee",
18425:       0};
18426:   verdigris::client::PresentationFx fx;
18427:   verdigris::client::apply_presentation_event(fx, mock.world, fake, 1);
18428:   const bool sim_has_attack = verdigris::client::qa::sim_emitted(
18429:       mock.simulation->events(), verdigris::EventType::AttackStarted);
18430:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::qa::mock_without_sim_rejected(sim_has_attack,
18431:                                                                  true),
18432:                  "headless-contract: a mocked event is not a journey proof");
18433:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::qa::journey_proved(sim_has_attack, true,
18434:                                                        !fx.effects.empty(), true),
18435:                  "headless-contract: swing FX from a mock cannot prove the journey");
18436: 
18437:   ClientState state;
18438:   scenario_begin(state);
18439:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18440:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
18441:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
18442:   bool saw_visual = false;
18443:   bool saw_audio = false;
18444:   for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
18445:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
18446:     drain_audio(state);
18447:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18448:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
18449:           (item.label == "intent:swing" || item.label == "headless-contract:ok" ||
18450:            item.label == "attack-beat:anticipate"))
18451:         saw_visual = true;
18452:     for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced)
18453:       if (cue == intent.audio_cue) saw_audio = true;
18454:   }
18455:   scenario_present(state);
18456:   drain_audio(state);
18457:   const bool sim_attack = verdigris::client::qa::sim_emitted(
18458:       state.simulation->events(), verdigris::EventType::AttackStarted);
18459:   bool live_visual = saw_visual;
18460:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18461:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
18462:         (item.label == "intent:swing" || item.label == "headless-contract:ok"))
18463:       live_visual = true;
18464:   for (const auto& cue : state.audio_voiced)
18465:     if (cue == intent.audio_cue) saw_audio = true;
18466:   scenario_check(sim_attack, "headless-contract: the simulation emitted AttackStarted");
18467:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::qa::journey_proved(sim_attack, bridge_mapped,
18468:                                                       live_visual, saw_audio),
18469:                  "headless-contract: real event reaches render and audio intent");
18470: 
18471:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18472:   if (dir.empty()) {
18473:     scenario_check(false, "headless-contract: capture root rejected before any write");
18474:     return scenario_failures;
18475:   }
18476:   const std::string png = dir + "\\headless-contract-960x600.png";
18477:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18478:                  "headless-contract: live contract capture written");
18479:   return scenario_failures;
18480: }
18481: 
18482: int scenario_input_latency() {
18483:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::input::command_time_is_not_photon(
18484:                      "command-dispatch-ms"),
18485:                  "input-latency: command time is not a photon label");
18486:   scenario_check(!verdigris::client::input::command_time_is_not_photon(
18487:                      verdigris::client::input::photon_kind_hud()),
18488:                  "input-latency: the photon label is rejected for this method");
18489: 
18490:   ClientState state;
18491:   scenario_begin(state);
18492:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18493:   LARGE_INTEGER freq{}, cmd_begin{}, cmd_end{};
18494:   QueryPerformanceFrequency(&freq);
18495:   QueryPerformanceCounter(&cmd_begin);
18496:   state.simulation->dispatch(verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
18497:   QueryPerformanceCounter(&cmd_end);
18498:   const double command_ms =
18499:       1000.0 * static_cast<double>(cmd_end.QuadPart - cmd_begin.QuadPart) /
18500:       static_cast<double>(freq.QuadPart);
18501: 
18502:   for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) {
18503:     verdigris::client::input::note_input(state.input_latency);
18504:     scenario_present(state);
18505:   }
18506:   scenario_present(state);
18507:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
18508:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18509:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
18510:     return false;
18511:   };
18512:   auto has_prefix = [&](const char* prefix) {
18513:     const std::string p = prefix;
18514:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18515:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind(p, 0) == 0)
18516:         return true;
18517:     return false;
18518:   };
18519:   scenario_check(state.input_latency.samples.size() >= 24,
18520:                  "input-latency: paired input-to-present samples were recorded");
18521:   const double p50 =
18522:       verdigris::client::input::percentile_ms(state.input_latency, 0.50);
18523:   const double p95 =
18524:       verdigris::client::input::percentile_ms(state.input_latency, 0.95);
18525:   scenario_check(p50 > 0.0 && p95 >= p50,
18526:                  "input-latency: p50/p95 are present-path milliseconds");
18527:   scenario_check(has_hud(verdigris::client::input::present_kind_hud()),
18528:                  "input-latency: the method is named present, not photon");
18529:   scenario_check(!has_hud(verdigris::client::input::photon_kind_hud()),
18530:                  "input-latency: photon must not appear on the HUD");
18531:   scenario_check(has_prefix("input-latency:p50:") && has_prefix("input-latency:p95:"),
18532:                  "input-latency: p50 and p95 are published");
18533:   SYSTEM_INFO sysinfo{};
18534:   GetNativeSystemInfo(&sysinfo);
18535:   std::printf("    input-latency machine: display %dx%d | %u logical CPUs | "
18536:               "OS Win32 | method input-to-present (not photon)\n",
18537:               GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN), GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN),
18538:               static_cast<unsigned>(sysinfo.dwNumberOfProcessors));
18539:   std::printf("    input-latency: p50 %.2f ms | p95 %.2f ms | n %zu | "
18540:               "command-dispatch %.3f ms (not photon)\n",
18541:               p50, p95, state.input_latency.samples.size(), command_ms);
18542: 
18543:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18544:   if (dir.empty()) {
18545:     scenario_check(false, "input-latency: capture root rejected before any write");
18546:     return scenario_failures;
18547:   }
18548:   const std::string png = dir + "\\input-latency-960x600.png";
18549:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18550:                  "input-latency: present-path capture written");
18551:   const std::string report = dir + "\\input-latency-report.txt";
18552:   FILE* out = nullptr;
18553:   fopen_s(&out, report.c_str(), "w");
18554:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "input-latency: report file opened");
18555:   if (out) {
18556:     std::fprintf(out,
18557:                  "method=input-to-present\nphoton=rejected\np50_ms=%.3f\n"
18558:                  "p95_ms=%.3f\nn=%zu\ncommand_dispatch_ms=%.4f\n",
18559:                  p50, p95, state.input_latency.samples.size(), command_ms);
18560:     std::fclose(out);
18561:   }
18562:   return scenario_failures;
18563: }

18565: int scenario_eight_way() {
18566:   scenario_check(verdigris::client::move::all_eight_encode(),
18567:                  "eight-way: all eight vectors encode without dropping an axis");
18568:   const std::string diag =
18569:       verdigris::client::move::encode_eight_way(-1, -1);
18570:   const std::string collapsed =
18571:       verdigris::client::move::collapse_to_vertical(-1, -1);
18572:   scenario_check(diag == "up-left", "eight-way: northwest is up-left on the wire");
18573:   scenario_check(collapsed == "up" && collapsed != diag,
18574:                  "eight-way: a vertical-only encoder cannot pass a diagonal");
18575:   for (const auto& vec : verdigris::client::move::kEightWay) {
18576:     const std::string name =
18577:         verdigris::client::move::encode_eight_way(vec[0], vec[1]);
18578:     scenario_check(verdigris::client::move::diagonal_keeps_both_axes(
18579:                        vec[0], vec[1], name),
18580:                    "eight-way: diagonal names keep both axes");
18581:   }
18582: 
18583:   ClientState state;
18584:   scenario_begin(state);
18585:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18586:   scenario_present(state);
18587:   auto has_hud = [&](const char* name) {
18588:     for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18589:       if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == name) return true;
18590:     return false;
18591:   };
18592:   scenario_check(has_hud("move-dir:eight-way"),
18593:                  "eight-way: production HUD names the eight-way encoder");
18594: 
18595:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18596:   if (dir.empty()) {
18597:     scenario_check(false, "eight-way: capture root rejected before any write");
18598:     return scenario_failures;
18599:   }
18600:   const std::string png = dir + "\\eight-way-960x600.png";
18601:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18602:                  "eight-way: capture written");
18603:   return scenario_failures;
18604: }

18606: int scenario_aim_hold() {
18607:   ClientState state;
18608:   scenario_begin(state);
18609:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18610:   const auto* scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18611:   scenario_check(scion != nullptr, "aim-hold: session has a scion");
18612:   if (!scion) return scenario_failures;
18613:   const int start_x = scion->position.x;
18614:   state.simulation->dispatch(verdigris::Command::aim(1, 0));
18615:   scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18616:   scenario_check(scion && scion->facing.x > 0, "aim-hold: east aim is stored");
18617:   state.simulation->dispatch(verdigris::Command::move(-1, 0));
18618:   scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18619:   verdigris::client::move::AimHold hold{};
18620:   verdigris::client::move::remember_aim(hold, 1, 0);
18621:   scenario_check(scion && verdigris::client::move::move_clobbered_aim(
18622:                               hold, scion->facing.x, scion->facing.y),
18623:                  "aim-hold: core move would overwrite aim without the adapter");
18624:   state.simulation->dispatch(verdigris::Command::aim(1, 0));
18625:   scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18626:   scenario_check(scion && scion->facing.x > 0 && scion->position.x < start_x,
18627:                  "aim-hold: adapter restores east aim after west locomotion");
18628: 
18629:   RECT bounds{0, 0, 960, 600};
18630:   for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
18631:     state.simulation->dispatch(verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
18632:     state.simulation->dispatch(verdigris::Command::aim(1, 0));
18633:   }
18634:   scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18635:   sync_world(state);
18636:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18637:   const ScreenPoint stand =
18638:       project(state.camera, bounds, static_cast<double>(scion->position.x),
18639:               static_cast<double>(scion->position.y));
18640:   state.mouse.x = stand.x + 160;
18641:   state.mouse.y = stand.y;
18642:   dispatch_aim_if_changed(state, bounds, true);
18643:   state.last_aim_direction = {1, 0};
18644:   state.aim_direction_initialized = true;
18645:   state.a = true;
18646:   state.d = false;
18647:   state.w = false;
18648:   state.s = false;
18649:   const int tick_x = scion->position.x;
18650:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) fixed_game_tick(state, bounds);
18651:   scion = state.simulation->actor(state.simulation->scion().actor_id);
18652:   scenario_check(scion && scion->facing.x > 0,
18653:                  "aim-hold: production tick keeps east aim while walking west");
18654:   scenario_check(scion && scion->position.x < tick_x,
18655:                  "aim-hold: west WASD still displaces");
18656:   scenario_present(state);
18657:   bool aim_hud = false;
18658:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
18659:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "aim-hold:right")
18660:       aim_hud = true;
18661:   scenario_check(aim_hud, "aim-hold: HUD names the held aim");
18662: 
18663:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18664:   if (dir.empty()) {
18665:     scenario_check(false, "aim-hold: capture root rejected before any write");
18666:     return scenario_failures;
18667:   }
18668:   const std::string png = dir + "\\aim-hold-960x600.png";
18669:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18670:                  "aim-hold: capture written");
18671:   return scenario_failures;
18672: }
18673: 
18674: verdigris::client::ui::ChannelMean sample_rect_channels(const void* bits, int width,
18675:                                                        int height, int cx, int cy) {
18676:   verdigris::client::ui::ChannelMean mean;
18677:   if (!bits) return mean;
18678:   const auto* p = static_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(bits);
18679:   const int x0 = std::max(0, cx - 4);
18680:   const int x1 = std::min(width, cx + 5);
18681:   const int y0 = std::max(0, cy - 12);
18682:   const int y1 = std::min(height, cy - 2);
18683:   long r = 0;
18684:   long b = 0;
18685:   for (int y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {
18686:     for (int x = x0; x < x1; ++x) {
18687:       const int i = (y * width + x) * 4;
18688:       const int blue = p[i + 2];
18689:       const int green = p[i + 1];
18690:       const int red = p[i];
18691:       if (red + green + blue < 48) continue;
18692:       r += red;
18693:       b += blue;
18694:       ++mean.samples;
18695:     }
18696:   }
18697:   if (mean.samples > 0) {
18698:     mean.r = static_cast<int>(r / mean.samples);
18699:     mean.b = static_cast<int>(b / mean.samples);
18700:   }
18701:   return mean;
18702: }
18703: 
18704: int scenario_vital_orbs() {
18705:   using verdigris::client::ui::life_on_screen_left;
18706:   using verdigris::client::ui::life_reads_red;
18707:   using verdigris::client::ui::mana_reads_blue;
18708:   using verdigris::client::ui::mute_on_mana_globe_fails;
18709:   using verdigris::client::ui::swapped_sheet_crops_fail;
18710:   scenario_check(swapped_sheet_crops_fail(kOrbMana.art.left),
18711:                  "vital-orbs: the blue crop cannot count as life");
18712:   scenario_check(!swapped_sheet_crops_fail(kOrbLife.art.left),
18713:                  "vital-orbs: life uses the red crop on the sheet");
18714: 
18715:   ClientState state;
18716:   scenario_begin(state);
18717:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18718:   scenario_check(state.audio_sink && state.audio_sink->muted(),
18719:                  "vital-orbs: scenarios still mute the sink");
18720: 
18721:   const int width = 960;
18722:   const int height = 600;
18723:   BITMAPINFO info{};
18724:   info.bmiHeader.biSize = sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER);
18725:   info.bmiHeader.biWidth = width;
18726:   info.bmiHeader.biHeight = -height;
18727:   info.bmiHeader.biPlanes = 1;
18728:   info.bmiHeader.biBitCount = 32;
18729:   info.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB;
18730:   void* bits = nullptr;
18731:   HBITMAP bitmap = CreateDIBSection(nullptr, &info, DIB_RGB_COLORS, &bits, nullptr, 0);
18732:   scenario_check(bitmap != nullptr, "vital-orbs: offscreen bitmap");
18733:   if (!bitmap) return scenario_failures;
18734:   HDC dc = CreateCompatibleDC(nullptr);
18735:   HGDIOBJ old = SelectObject(dc, bitmap);
18736:   RECT bounds{0, 0, width, height};
18737:   paint_scene(state, dc, bounds);
18738: 
18739:   int life_cx = 0;
18740:   int life_cy = 0;
18741:   int mana_cx = 0;
18742:   int mana_cy = 0;
18743:   bool life_role = false;
18744:   bool mana_role = false;
18745:   bool mute_chip = false;
18746:   bool mute_on_mana = false;
18747:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
18748:     if (item.op == render::Op::Orb && item.label == "life") {
18749:       life_cx = static_cast<int>(item.x);
18750:       life_cy = static_cast<int>(item.y);
18751:     }
18752:     if (item.op == render::Op::Orb && item.label == "resource") {
18753:       mana_cx = static_cast<int>(item.x);
18754:       mana_cy = static_cast<int>(item.y);
18755:     }
18756:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "orb-role:life-left")
18757:       life_role = true;
18758:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "orb-role:mana-right")
18759:       mana_role = true;
18760:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "audio:muted") {
18761:       mute_chip = true;
18762:       mute_on_mana = std::abs(static_cast<int>(item.x) - mana_cx) < 24;
18763:     }
18764:   }
18765:   scenario_check(life_on_screen_left(life_cx, mana_cx),
18766:                  "vital-orbs: life orb is left of mana");
18767:   scenario_check(life_role && mana_role, "vital-orbs: HUD names constitution roles");
18768:   scenario_check(mute_chip, "vital-orbs: mute still has a non-color cue");
18769:   scenario_check(!mute_on_mana_globe_fails(true, mute_on_mana),
18770:                  "vital-orbs: mute cannot sit on the mana globe");
18771: 
18772:   const auto life_ch =
18773:       sample_rect_channels(bits, width, height, life_cx, life_cy);
18774:   const auto mana_ch =
18775:       sample_rect_channels(bits, width, height, mana_cx, mana_cy);
18776:   std::printf("    vital-orbs: life rgb r=%d b=%d n=%d | mana r=%d b=%d n=%d\n",
18777:               life_ch.r, life_ch.b, life_ch.samples, mana_ch.r, mana_ch.b,
18778:               mana_ch.samples);
18779:   scenario_check(life_reads_red(life_ch),
18780:                  "vital-orbs: left globe reads red, not blue");
18781:   scenario_check(mana_reads_blue(mana_ch),
18782:                  "vital-orbs: right globe reads blue, not orange");
18783:   scenario_check(verdigris::gpu::swapped_png_rejected(life_ch.b, life_ch.r, life_ch.r,
18784:                                                     life_ch.b),
18785:                  "vital-orbs: an R/B swapped still cannot certify life");
18786: 
18787:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18788:   if (dir.empty()) {
18789:     scenario_check(false, "vital-orbs: capture root rejected before any write");
18790:     SelectObject(dc, old);
18791:     DeleteDC(dc);
18792:     DeleteObject(bitmap);
18793:     return scenario_failures;
18794:   }
18795:   const std::string png = dir + "\\vital-orbs-960x600.png";
18796:   scenario_check(save_hbitmap_png(state.billboards, bitmap, png),
18797:                  "vital-orbs: capture written");
18798:   SelectObject(dc, old);
18799:   DeleteDC(dc);
18800:   DeleteObject(bitmap);
18801:   return scenario_failures;
18802: }

18804: int scenario_death_disconnect() {
18805:   using verdigris::client::gov::Carry;
18806:   using verdigris::client::gov::EndEvent;
18807:   using verdigris::client::gov::extract_hud;
18808:   using verdigris::client::gov::paints_extract_ok;
18809:   using verdigris::client::gov::silent_disconnect_ack;
18810:   scenario_check(silent_disconnect_ack(EndEvent::Disconnect, Carry::Uncommitted),
18811:                  "death-disconnect: disconnect+uncommitted is the negative");
18812:   scenario_check(!paints_extract_ok(EndEvent::Disconnect, Carry::Uncommitted),
18813:                  "death-disconnect: that negative cannot paint extract:ok");
18814:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(extract_hud(EndEvent::Disconnect, Carry::Uncommitted),
18815:                              "extract:uncommitted") == 0,
18816:                  "death-disconnect: disconnect HUD names uncommitted carry");
18817:   scenario_check(paints_extract_ok(EndEvent::Disconnect, Carry::ExtractCommitted),
18818:                  "death-disconnect: already-banked extract:ok survives reconnect");
18819:   scenario_check(!silent_disconnect_ack(EndEvent::Disconnect, Carry::ExtractCommitted),
18820:                  "death-disconnect: committed extract is not a silent ack");
18821:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(extract_hud(EndEvent::Death, Carry::Uncommitted),
18822:                              "extract:uncommitted") == 0,
18823:                  "death-disconnect: death does not bank by HUD");
18824: 
18825:   ClientState state;
18826:   scenario_begin(state);
18827:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18828:   state.link_lost = true;
18829:   scenario_present(state);
18830:   bool uncommitted = false;
18831:   bool extract_ok = false;
18832:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
18833:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
18834:     if (item.label == "extract:uncommitted") uncommitted = true;
18835:     if (item.label == "extract:ok") extract_ok = true;
18836:   }
18837:   scenario_check(uncommitted,
18838:                  "death-disconnect: live HUD publishes extract:uncommitted");
18839:   scenario_check(!extract_ok,
18840:                  "death-disconnect: live HUD cannot ack an uncommitted extract");
18841: 
18842:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18843:   if (dir.empty()) {
18844:     scenario_check(false, "death-disconnect: capture root rejected before any write");
18845:     return scenario_failures;
18846:   }
18847:   const std::string png = dir + "\\death-disconnect-960x600.png";
18848:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18849:                  "death-disconnect: capture written");
18850:   return scenario_failures;
18851: }

18853: int scenario_route_map() {
18854:   using verdigris::client::ui::leaky_zoom_paints_off_snapshot;
18855:   using verdigris::client::ui::MapOverlay;
18856:   using verdigris::client::ui::MapTarget;
18857:   using verdigris::client::ui::overlay_mutates_world;
18858:   using verdigris::client::ui::overlay_paints_blip;
18859:   using verdigris::client::ui::route_return_fact;
18860:   using verdigris::client::ui::route_risk_fact;
18861: 
18862:   const MapOverlay tight{2, 255};
18863:   const MapTarget hidden{"off-snapshot-warden", true, false};
18864:   const MapTarget seen{"snapshot-warden", true, true};
18865:   scenario_check(leaky_zoom_paints_off_snapshot(2, false),
18866:                  "route-map: max zoom + off-snapshot is the leaky anti-pattern");
18867:   scenario_check(!overlay_paints_blip(tight, hidden),
18868:                  "route-map: production overlay cannot use that leak");
18869:   scenario_check(overlay_paints_blip(tight, seen),
18870:                  "route-map: snapshot-visible foes still paint at max zoom");
18871:   scenario_check(!overlay_mutates_world(tight),
18872:                  "route-map: overlay settings are not world writes");
18873:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(route_risk_fact(true, false), "risk wardens") == 0,
18874:                  "route-map: slay phase posts warden risk");
18875:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(route_return_fact(false), "return town") == 0,
18876:                  "route-map: no pad still returns to town");
18877:   using verdigris::client::ui::route_owner_title;
18878:   using verdigris::client::ui::route_theme_label;
18879:   scenario_check(route_owner_title("route:tin:1:0") == "Tin village",
18880:                  "route-map: tin root paints as Tin village");
18881:   scenario_check(route_owner_title("route:tin:1:0").find(':') == std::string::npos,
18882:                  "route-map: owner title cannot keep a protocol colon");
18883:   scenario_check(route_owner_title("not-a-wire-token") == "not-a-wire-token",
18884:                  "route-map: a display name without colons still shows");
18885:   scenario_check(route_theme_label("town") == "Town road",
18886:                  "route-map: town theme is a road name, not theme town");
18887:   using verdigris::client::ui::route_risk_owner_line;
18888:   using verdigris::client::ui::route_return_owner_line;
18889:   scenario_check(route_risk_owner_line("risk wardens") == "Risk: wardens",
18890:                  "route-map: owner risk line is Risk: wardens");
18891:   scenario_check(route_return_owner_line("return press F there") ==
18892:                      "Return: press F at the pad",
18893:                  "route-map: owner return line names the pad, not press F there");
18894: 
18895:   ClientState state;
18896:   scenario_begin(state);
18897:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18898:   const verdigris::Vec2 before = state.world.player.position;
18899:   WorldActor ghost;
18900:   ghost.id = "off-snapshot-warden";
18901:   ghost.alive = true;
18902:   ghost.on_snapshot = false;
18903:   ghost.position = {before.x + 96, before.y};
18904:   state.map_overlay_probes.push_back(ghost);
18905:   WorldActor listed;
18906:   listed.id = "snapshot-warden";
18907:   listed.alive = true;
18908:   listed.on_snapshot = true;
18909:   listed.position = {before.x + 48, before.y};
18910:   state.map_overlay_probes.push_back(listed);
18911:   state.world.expedition_phase = ExpeditionPhaseView::SlayWardens;
18912:   state.minimap_zoom = 2;
18913:   state.minimap_opacity = 255;
18914:   scenario_present(state);
18915: 
18916:   scenario_check(state.world.player.position.x == before.x &&
18917:                      state.world.player.position.y == before.y,
18918:                  "route-map: zoom/opacity cannot move the player");
18919:   bool leaked = false;
18920:   bool listed_blip = false;
18921:   bool zoom_hud = false;
18922:   bool opacity_hud = false;
18923:   bool risk_hud = false;
18924:   bool route_hud = false;
18925:   bool ghost_named = false;
18926:   bool protocol_title = false;
18927:   bool tin_village = false;
18928:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
18929:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
18930:     if (item.label == "map-blip:off-snapshot-warden") leaked = true;
18931:     if (item.label == "map-blip:snapshot-warden") listed_blip = true;
18932:     if (item.label == "minimap-zoom:2") zoom_hud = true;
18933:     if (item.label == "map-opacity:255") opacity_hud = true;
18934:     if (item.label == "route-risk:risk wardens") risk_hud = true;
18935:     if (item.label.rfind("route:", 0) == 0) route_hud = true;
18936:     if (item.label == "route:Tin village") tin_village = true;
18937:     if (item.label.find("route:tin:") != std::string::npos ||
18938:         item.label.find("route:salt:") != std::string::npos)
18939:       protocol_title = true;
18940:     if (item.label.find("off-snapshot-warden") != std::string::npos)
18941:       ghost_named = true;
18942:   }
18943:   scenario_check(!leaked,
18944:                  "route-map: tight zoom cannot mint an off-snapshot blip");
18945:   scenario_check(listed_blip,
18946:                  "route-map: a snapshot foe still has a map blip");
18947:   scenario_check(zoom_hud && opacity_hud,
18948:                  "route-map: zoom and opacity publish as overlay settings");
18949:   scenario_check(route_hud && risk_hud,
18950:                  "route-map: route card names return/risk without foe ids");
18951:   scenario_check(tin_village,
18952:                  "route-map: live card titles Tin village, not the wire id");
18953:   scenario_check(!protocol_title,
18954:                  "route-map: a protocol route id cannot be the owner title");
18955:   scenario_check(!ghost_named,
18956:                  "route-map: HUD cannot name a server-hidden target");
18957: 
18958:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
18959:   if (dir.empty()) {
18960:     scenario_check(false, "route-map: capture root rejected before any write");
18961:     return scenario_failures;
18962:   }
18963:   const std::string png = dir + "\\route-map-960x600.png";
18964:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
18965:                  "route-map: capture written");
18966:   return scenario_failures;
18967: }

18969: int scenario_stat_explain() {
18970:   using verdigris::client::ui::StatSources;
18971:   using verdigris::client::ui::active_attack;
18972:   using verdigris::client::ui::folds_dormant_into_attack;
18973:   StatSources src{12, 5, 3, 9, false, true};
18974:   scenario_check(active_attack(src) == 20,
18975:                  "stat-explain: active total is base+gear+passive");
18976:   scenario_check(folds_dormant_into_attack(src, 29),
18977:                  "stat-explain: folding dormant 9 into Attack is the anti-pattern");
18978:   scenario_check(!folds_dormant_into_attack(src, 20),
18979:                  "stat-explain: the live total must reject that fold");
18980: 
18981:   ClientState state;
18982:   scenario_begin(state);
18983:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
18984:   state.character_pane = true;
18985:   scenario_present(state);
18986:   const int base = state.world.player.attack;
18987:   int gear = 0;
18988:   for (const auto& item : state.world.carried)
18989:     if (item.equipped) gear = item.attack_bonus;
18990:   state.sheet_passive_atk = 3;
18991:   state.sheet_cond_atk = 9;
18992:   state.sheet_cond_active = false;
18993:   state.stat_atk_expanded = false;
18994:   scenario_present(state);
18995:   const int expect = active_attack(
18996:       {base, gear, 3, 9, false, false});
18997:   bool collapsed_ok = false;
18998:   bool folded = false;
18999:   bool dormant = false;
19000:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19001:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
19002:     if (item.label.rfind("char:Attack:", 0) == 0) {
19003:       collapsed_ok = item.label == ("char:Attack:" + std::to_string(expect));
19004:       folded = item.label == ("char:Attack:" + std::to_string(expect + 9));
19005:     }
19006:     if (item.label.rfind("char:Cond:9 · inactive", 0) == 0) dormant = true;
19007:   }
19008:   scenario_check(collapsed_ok, "stat-explain: collapsed Attack excludes dormant");
19009:   scenario_check(!folded, "stat-explain: dormant 9 cannot appear as Attack");
19010:   scenario_check(dormant, "stat-explain: conditional stays labeled inactive");
19011: 
19012:   state.stat_atk_expanded = true;
19013:   scenario_present(state);
19014:   bool src_base = false, src_gear = false, src_passive = false, src_cond = false;
19015:   bool expanded = false, excluded = false;
19016:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19017:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
19018:     if (item.label.rfind("char:src base:", 0) == 0) src_base = true;
19019:     if (item.label.rfind("char:src gear:", 0) == 0) src_gear = true;
19020:     if (item.label.rfind("char:src passive:", 0) == 0) src_passive = true;
19021:     if (item.label.rfind("char:src cond:", 0) == 0) src_cond = true;
19022:     if (item.label == "char:atk-expanded:1") expanded = true;
19023:     if (item.label == "char:atk-dormant-excluded") excluded = true;
19024:   }
19025:   scenario_check(src_base && src_gear && src_passive && src_cond,
19026:                  "stat-explain: expanded sheet names base, gear, passive, cond");
19027:   scenario_check(expanded && excluded,
19028:                  "stat-explain: expand flag and dormant-exclusion are on the HUD");
19029:   bool owner_labels = false;
19030:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
19031:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "stat:owner-labels")
19032:       owner_labels = true;
19033:   scenario_check(owner_labels,
19034:                  "stat-explain: expanded sources paint Base/Gear, not src jargon");
19035: 
19036:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
19037:   if (dir.empty()) {
19038:     scenario_check(false, "stat-explain: capture root rejected before any write");
19039:     return scenario_failures;
19040:   }
19041:   const std::string png = dir + "\\stat-explain-960x600.png";
19042:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
19043:                  "stat-explain: capture written");
19044:   return scenario_failures;
19045: }
19046: 
19047: int scenario_held_item() {
19048:   using verdigris::client::art::paper_doll_only_fails_review;
19049:   using verdigris::client::art::world_shows_attachment;
19050:   scenario_check(paper_doll_only_fails_review(true, false),
19051:                  "held-item: a filled seat with no world hold is the anti-pattern");
19052:   scenario_check(!world_shows_attachment("held:none", 0),
19053:                  "held-item: held:none is not a world attachment");
19054:   scenario_check(world_shows_attachment("held:sword", 2),
19055:                  "held-item: a non-none held enum is a world attachment");
19056:   scenario_check(!paper_doll_only_fails_review(true, true),
19057:                  "held-item: seat plus world hold can pass review");
19058: 
19059:   ClientState state;
19060:   scenario_begin(state);
19061:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
19062:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
19063:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
19064:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
19065:   if (!state.simulation->ground_items().empty())
19066:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::pick_up(
19067:                              state.simulation->ground_items().front().id));
19068:   scenario_check(!state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty(),
19069:                  "held-item: pickup yields a carried weapon");
19070:   scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::unequip());
19071:   state.gear_overlay = true;
19072:   scenario_present(state);
19073:   bool unarmed_world = false;
19074:   bool empty_seat = false;
19075:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19076:     if (item.op == render::Op::Player && item.label == "held:none")
19077:       unarmed_world = true;
19078:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "held-seat:(empty)")
19079:       empty_seat = true;
19080:   }
19081:   scenario_check(unarmed_world, "held-item: unequip clears the world attachment");
19082:   scenario_check(empty_seat, "held-item: unequip clears the paper-doll seat");
19083: 
19084:   if (!state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty())
19085:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::equip(
19086:                              state.simulation->scion().carried_items.front().id));
19087:   state.gear_overlay = true;
19088:   scenario_present(state);
19089:   bool world_held = false;
19090:   bool seat_filled = false;
19091:   bool world_hud = false;
19092:   const render::Item* scion = render::first(state.render_list, render::Op::Player);
19093:   if (scion)
19094:     world_held = world_shows_attachment(scion->label.c_str(), scion->value);
19095:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19096:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("held-seat:", 0) == 0 &&
19097:         item.label != "held-seat:(empty)")
19098:       seat_filled = true;
19099:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("held-world:", 0) == 0 &&
19100:         item.label != "held-world:held:none")
19101:       world_hud = true;
19102:   }
19103:   scenario_check(world_held, "held-item: ack equip changes the world actor layer");
19104:   scenario_check(seat_filled, "held-item: paper-doll names the equipped weapon");
19105:   scenario_check(world_hud, "held-item: HUD publishes the world hold, not only the seat");
19106:   scenario_check(!paper_doll_only_fails_review(seat_filled, world_held),
19107:                  "held-item: paper-doll alone cannot satisfy the journey");
19108: 
19109:   state.gear_overlay = false;
19110:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
19111:   if (dir.empty()) {
19112:     scenario_check(false, "held-item: capture root rejected before any write");
19113:     return scenario_failures;
19114:   }
19115:   const std::string png = dir + "\\held-item-960x600.png";
19116:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
19117:                  "held-item: world-hold capture written");
19118:   return scenario_failures;
19119: }
19120: 
19121: int scenario_pack_drag() {
19122:   using verdigris::client::ui::classify_pack_drop;
19123:   using verdigris::client::ui::PackDrop;
19124:   using verdigris::client::ui::pack_drop_hud;
19125:   using verdigris::client::ui::reject_loses_or_duplicates;
19126:   using verdigris::client::ui::reject_silently_equips;
19127:   scenario_check(classify_pack_drop(true, false, false, true) == PackDrop::Reject,
19128:                  "pack-drag: a failed preview is a reject");
19129:   scenario_check(reject_loses_or_duplicates(PackDrop::Reject, 1, 0),
19130:                  "pack-drag: losing the item on reject is the anti-pattern");
19131:   scenario_check(reject_silently_equips(PackDrop::Reject, true),
19132:                  "pack-drag: silent equip on reject is the anti-pattern");
19133:   scenario_check(!reject_loses_or_duplicates(PackDrop::Reject, 1, 1),
19134:                  "pack-drag: production reject keeps the carried count");
19135:   scenario_check(std::strcmp(pack_drop_hud(PackDrop::Ok), "pack-drop:ok") == 0,
19136:                  "pack-drag: HUD names a successful place");
19137: 
19138:   ClientState state;
19139:   scenario_begin(state);
19140:   for (int i = 0; i < 52; ++i)
19141:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::move(1, 0));
19142:   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
19143:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::action_use(verdigris::ActionType::Melee));
19144:   if (!state.simulation->ground_items().empty())
19145:     scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::pick_up(
19146:                              state.simulation->ground_items().front().id));
19147:   scenario_check(!state.simulation->scion().carried_items.empty(),
19148:                  "pack-drag: pickup fills the pack");
19149:   scenario_step(state, verdigris::Command::unequip());
19150:   state.gear_overlay = true;
19151:   scenario_present(state);
19152:   scenario_check(state.pack_grid.count > 0, "pack-drag: grid has the carried item");
19153:   const std::uint32_t pid = state.pack_grid.items[0].id;
19154:   const int carried_n = static_cast<int>(state.world.carried.size());
19155:   pack_begin_drag(state, state.pack_grid.items[0].x, state.pack_grid.items[0].y);
19156:   state.pack_preview_x = 2;
19157:   state.pack_preview_y = 1;
19158:   state.pack_preview_ok = pack_can_land(state.pack_grid, pid, 2, 1);
19159:   pack_commit_drop(state, false);
19160:   scenario_check(state.pack_last_drop == "ok" &&
19161:                      inventory_grid::item_at(state.pack_grid, 2, 1) == pid,
19162:                  "pack-drag: valid drop moves the item");
19163:   scenario_present(state);
19164:   bool moved = false;
19165:   bool drop_ok = false;
19166:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19167:     if (item.op != render::Op::Hud) continue;
19168:     if (item.label == "pack:2,1") moved = true;
19169:     if (item.label == "pack-drop:ok") drop_ok = true;
19170:   }
19171:   scenario_check(moved && drop_ok, "pack-drag: moved cell and drop HUD are painted");
19172: 
19173:   pack_begin_drag(state, 2, 1);
19174:   state.pack_preview_x = 20;
19175:   state.pack_preview_y = 20;
19176:   state.pack_preview_ok = false;
19177:   pack_commit_drop(state, false);
19178:   bool equipped = false;
19179:   for (const auto& item : state.world.carried)
19180:     if (item.equipped) equipped = true;
19181:   scenario_check(state.pack_last_drop == "reject" &&
19182:                      inventory_grid::item_at(state.pack_grid, 2, 1) == pid &&
19183:                      static_cast<int>(state.world.carried.size()) == carried_n,
19184:                  "pack-drag: reject neither loses nor duplicates");
19185:   scenario_check(!equipped, "pack-drag: reject does not silently equip");
19186:   scenario_check(!reject_loses_or_duplicates(
19187:                      PackDrop::Reject, carried_n,
19188:                      static_cast<int>(state.world.carried.size())),
19189:                  "pack-drag: reject occupancy matches the helper");
19190:   scenario_present(state);
19191:   bool drop_reject = false;
19192:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
19193:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label == "pack-drop:reject")
19194:       drop_reject = true;
19195:   scenario_check(drop_reject, "pack-drag: reject is visible on the gear HUD");
19196:   bool pack_glyph = false;
19197:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19198:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud &&
19199:         (item.label == "pack-glyph:vector" ||
19200:          item.label == "pack-glyph:billboard"))
19201:       pack_glyph = true;

19206:                      vector_art::kPackGlyphHasBlade,

19208:                  "pack-drag: a grey square cannot certify a carried weapon");
19209: 
19210:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
19211:   if (dir.empty()) {
19212:     scenario_check(false, "pack-drag: capture root rejected before any write");
19213:     return scenario_failures;
19214:   }
19215:   const std::string png = dir + "\\pack-drag-960x600.png";
19216:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
19217:                  "pack-drag: capture written");
19218:   return scenario_failures;
19219: }

19221: int scenario_resource_envelope() {
19222:   ClientState state;
19223:   scenario_begin(state);
19224:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
19225:   scenario_present_size(state, 960, 600);
19226:   const PresentationResources first = presentation_resources(state);
19227:   scenario_check(first.floor_bitmaps == 1,
19228:                  "resource-envelope: one floor bitmap after the first paint");
19229:   int max_floor_w = first.floor_w;
19230:   for (int cycle = 0; cycle < 8; ++cycle) {
19231:     scenario_present_size(state, 1920, 1080);
19232:     scenario_present_size(state, 640, 400);
19233:     scenario_present_size(state, 960, 600);
19234:     const PresentationResources mid = presentation_resources(state);
19235:     max_floor_w = std::max(max_floor_w, mid.floor_w);
19236:     scenario_check(mid.floor_bitmaps == 1,
19237:                    "resource-envelope: resize cycles keep a single floor bitmap");
19238:     scenario_check(mid.gdi_pens <= static_cast<int>(kMaxCachedPens),
19239:                    "resource-envelope: GDI pens stay inside the 128 cap");
19240:     scenario_check(mid.gdi_brushes <= static_cast<int>(kMaxCachedBrushes),
19241:                    "resource-envelope: GDI brushes stay inside the 128 cap");
19242:   }
19243:   const PresentationResources settled = presentation_resources(state);
19244:   scenario_check(settled.floor_w <= first.floor_w * 2,
19245:                  "resource-envelope: returning to 960 shrinks an oversized floor");
19246:   scenario_check(settled.floor_w <= max_floor_w,
19247:                  "resource-envelope: floor width is not a second leaked bitmap");
19248: 
19249:   for (int i = 0; i < 300; ++i) {
19250:     EffectFx fx;
19251:     fx.kind = EffectFx::Kind::Impact;
19252:     fx.wx = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.x + i);
19253:     fx.wy = static_cast<double>(state.world.player.position.y);
19254:     fx.ttl = 8;
19255:     add_effect(state, fx);
19256:   }
19257:   scenario_present(state);
19258:   const PresentationResources flooded = presentation_resources(state);
19259:   std::printf("    resource-envelope: floor %dx%d (1 bitmap) | pens %d | "
19260:               "brushes %d | fx %d | paint %.1f ms\n",
19261:               flooded.floor_w, flooded.floor_h, flooded.gdi_pens,
19262:               flooded.gdi_brushes, flooded.effects, state.last_paint_ms);
19263:   scenario_check(flooded.effects <= static_cast<int>(kMaxPresentationEffects),
19264:                  "resource-envelope: 300 spawned effects stay at the 128 cap");
19265:   scenario_check(state.last_paint_ms < 40.0,
19266:                  "resource-envelope: a cheap frame does not excuse a growing "
19267:                  "effect list");
19268:   scenario_check(flooded.effects == static_cast<int>(kMaxPresentationEffects),
19269:                  "resource-envelope: the cap is occupied, not emptied to pass");
19270:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
19271:   if (dir.empty()) {
19272:     scenario_check(false, "resource-envelope: capture root rejected before any write");
19273:     return scenario_failures;
19274:   }
19275:   const std::string report = dir + "\\resource-envelope-report.txt";
19276:   FILE* out = nullptr;
19277:   fopen_s(&out, report.c_str(), "w");
19278:   scenario_check(out != nullptr, "resource-envelope: report opened");
19279:   if (out) {
19280:     std::fprintf(out,
19281:                  "floor_w=%d\nfloor_h=%d\nfloor_bitmaps=%d\npens=%d\nbrushes=%d\n"
19282:                  "fx=%d\npaint_ms=%.3f\n",
19283:                  flooded.floor_w, flooded.floor_h, flooded.floor_bitmaps,
19284:                  flooded.gdi_pens, flooded.gdi_brushes, flooded.effects,
19285:                  state.last_paint_ms);
19286:     std::fclose(out);
19287:   }
19288:   const std::string png = dir + "\\resource-envelope-960x600.png";
19289:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
19290:                  "resource-envelope: capped-effect capture written");
19291:   return scenario_failures;
19292: }
19293: 
19294: int scenario_loot_label_budget() {
19295:   ClientState state;
19296:   scenario_begin(state);
19297:   scenario_follow_camera(state);
19298:   const verdigris::Vec2 origin = state.world.player.position;
19299:   state.loot_labels = true;
19300:   constexpr int kDense = 120;
19301:   for (int i = 0; i < kDense; ++i) {
19302:     char id[40];
19303:     std::snprintf(id, sizeof(id), "dense-drop-%03d", i);
19304:     state.loot_positions[id] = {origin.x + (i % 20) * 24, origin.y + (i / 20) * 24};
19305:   }
19306:   scenario_present(state);
19307:   int drops = 0;
19308:   int labels = 0;
19309:   bool far_sprite = false;
19310:   bool far_named = false;
19311:   bool near_named = false;
19312:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list) {
19313:     if (item.op == render::Op::Drop) {
19314:       ++drops;
19315:       if (item.label == "dense-drop-119") far_sprite = true;
19316:     }
19317:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("loot-label:", 0) == 0) {
19318:       ++labels;
19319:       if (item.label == "loot-label:dense-drop-119") far_named = true;
19320:       if (item.label == "loot-label:dense-drop-000") near_named = true;
19321:     }
19322:   }
19323:   scenario_check(drops >= kDense,
19324:                  "loot-label-budget: every dense pouch still draws as Drop");
19325:   scenario_check(far_sprite,
19326:                  "loot-label-budget: a far pouch remains a Drop sprite");
19327:   scenario_check(!far_named,
19328:                  "loot-label-budget: the farthest pouch is not nameplated");
19329:   scenario_check(near_named,
19330:                  "loot-label-budget: the nearest pouch keeps a readable name");
19331:   scenario_check(labels > 0,
19332:                  "loot-label-budget: hiding every name to pass the cap fails");
19333:   scenario_check(labels <= static_cast<int>(kMaxLootNameplates),
19334:                  "loot-label-budget: nameplates stay at the 12-nearest cap");
19335: 
19336:   state.loot_labels = false;
19337:   scenario_present(state);
19338:   int silent_labels = 0;
19339:   for (const auto& item : state.render_list)
19340:     if (item.op == render::Op::Hud && item.label.rfind("loot-label:", 0) == 0)
19341:       ++silent_labels;
19342:   scenario_check(silent_labels == 0,
19343:                  "loot-label-budget: labels off means zero loot-label ops");
19344:   state.loot_labels = true;
19345:   const std::string dir = art_wave_capture_dir();
19346:   if (dir.empty()) {
19347:     scenario_check(false, "loot-label-budget: capture root rejected before any write");
19348:     return scenario_failures;
19349:   }
19350:   const std::string png = dir + "\\loot-label-budget-960x600.png";
19351:   scenario_check(reference_present(state, 960, 600, png),
19352:                  "loot-label-budget: capped nameplate capture written");
19353:   return scenario_failures;
--- context after ---
19355: }
